                            <textarea
                                name="description"
                                value={formData.description || ''}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                rows={3}
                                className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                                placeholder="Recursos incluídos no plano..."
                            />
                        </div>
                        
                        {/* Campo Status (Apenas Edição) */}
                        {isEditing && (
                            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 pt-2">
                                <input
                                    id="isActive"
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    name="isActive"
                                    checked={formData.isActive}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    className="h-4 w-4 text-accent-secondary border-gray-600 rounded"
                                />
                                <label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">
                                    Plano Ativo (Permitir novos Tenants)
                                </label>
                            </div>
                        )}
                        
                        {/* Botão de Ação */}
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full flex justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 transition disabled:bg-gray-500"
                        >
                            {isLoading ? 'Salvando Detalhes...' : (isEditing ? 'Salvar Detalhes' : 'Criar Plano')}
                        </button>
                    </form>
                )}

                {/* ABA 2: GERENCIAMENTO DE REGRAS */}
                {currentTab === 'RULES' && isEditing && currentPlan && (
                    <PlanRuleManager 
                        planoId={currentPlan.id} 
                        onSyncSuccess={onSuccess} // Usa o callback principal do modal
                    />
                )}
                {/* --------------------------------------------- */}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/admin/components/PlanRuleManager.tsx =====

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaSave, FaTag, FaCalculator } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useFeatures } from '@/core/hooks/useFeatures';
import { usePlanRules } from '@/core/hooks/usePlanRules';
import { usePlanMutation } from '@/core/hooks/usePlanMutation';
import { Feature, FeatureType, PlanFeatureRule } from '@/core/types/acl';

// Propriedades do Manager
interface PlanRuleManagerProps {
    planoId: string;
    onSyncSuccess: () => void; // Callback para o Modal
}

// Tipo de Estado da Regra (Combina Feature + Valor Atual)
interface RuleState {
    feature: Feature;
    limitValue: number;
    // O valor -1 será usado para representar o status "Ativo/Ilimitado" para booleanas ou quantity.
}

/**
 * @component PlanRuleManager
 * @description Interface para gerenciar as regras (PlanFeature) de um Plano específico.
 * Porquê: Centraliza a lógica de mapeamento, edição de limites e chamada transacional de sincronização.
 */
export const PlanRuleManager: React.FC<PlanRuleManagerProps> = ({ planoId, onSyncSuccess }) => {
    
    // Hooks de Leitura
    const { features, isLoading: loadingFeatures, error: featuresError } = useFeatures();
    const { rules, isLoading: loadingRules, error: rulesError, fetchRules } = usePlanRules(planoId);
    
    // Hooks de Mutação
    const { syncPlanRules, isLoading: isSyncing, error: syncError } = usePlanMutation();

    // Estado principal que armazena a lista de regras para edição
    const [ruleState, setRuleState] = useState<RuleState[]>([]);
    
    // --- LÓGICA DE MAPEAR REGRAS ---
    
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!loadingFeatures && features.length > 0) {
            // Mapeia todas as Features disponíveis (features)
            const initialRules: RuleState[] = features.map(feature => {
                // Encontra a regra atual (rules) para esta feature
                const currentRule = rules.find(r => r.featureId === feature.id);
                
                // Define o valor limite inicial:
                let initialLimit = 0; // Default: Inativo/Não permitido
                
                if (currentRule) {
                    initialLimit = currentRule.limitValue;
                } else if (feature.type === FeatureType.BOOLEAN) {
                    // Se a feature for booleana e não tem regra, setamos 0 (Inativo)
                    initialLimit = 0;
                }
                
                return {
                    feature,
                    limitValue: initialLimit,
                };
            });

            setRuleState(initialRules);
        }
    }, [features, rules, loadingFeatures, loadingRules]); // Dependências de recarregamento
    
    // --- FUNÇÕES DE AÇÃO E EDIÇÃO ---

    const handleLimitChange = (featureId: string, value: string) => {
        // Converte a entrada de texto (quantity) para número
        const numericValue = parseInt(value, 10);
        
        setRuleState(prev => prev.map(item => {
            if (item.feature.id === featureId) {
                return {
                    ...item,
                    // Garante que o valor seja um número válido ou -1 (Ilimitado)
                    limitValue: isNaN(numericValue) ? 0 : numericValue, 
                };
            }
            return item;
        }));
    };

    const handleBooleanToggle = (featureId: string, isActive: boolean) => {
        // Para booleanas, 1 é Ativo, 0 é Inativo
        const value = isActive ? 1 : 0;
        
        setRuleState(prev => prev.map(item => {
            if (item.feature.id === featureId) {
                return {
                    ...item,
                    limitValue: value,
                };
            }
            return item;
        }));
    };

    const handleSyncRules = async () => {
        // 1. Filtrar apenas as regras ATIVAS ou com LIMITES definidos (> 0 ou != 0)
        // Porquê: Não queremos enviar regras com limitValue = 0 ou null, 
        // a menos que seja uma booleana explícita.
        const rulesToSend: PlanFeatureRule[] = ruleState
            .filter(item => item.limitValue !== 0)
            .map(item => ({
                featureId: item.feature.id,
                limitValue: item.limitValue,
            }));

        try {
            // 2. Chamada de Sincronização Transacional
            await syncPlanRules(planoId, rulesToSend);
            alert("Regras do Plano salvas com sucesso!");
            
            // 3. Callback de sucesso e recarregamento da tela.
            await fetchRules(); // Revalida os dados da tabela de regras
            onSyncSuccess(); // Callback para o modal principal
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Falha na sincronização:", error);
        }
    };
    
    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DE ESTADO ---
    if (loadingFeatures || loadingRules) {
        return <div className="text-center text-gray-400 py-4">Carregando Features e Regras...</div>;
    }
    
    if (featuresError || rulesError || syncError) {
        return <div className="p-3 bg-danger/20 text-danger rounded-lg">Erro: {featuresError || rulesError || syncError}</div>;
    }

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DA TABELA DE REGRAS ---

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-base-text border-b border-gray-700 pb-2">
                Configuração de Limites por Feature
            </h3>
            
            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-gray-800 rounded-lg">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-700">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300">Feature</th>
                            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300">Tipo de Controle</th>
                            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 w-1/4">Valor / Acesso</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                        {ruleState.map(item => (
                            <tr key={item.feature.id}>
                                {/* Nome da Feature */}
                                <td className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div className="text-sm text-white">{item.feature.name}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">{item.feature.key}</div>
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Tipo de Controle (Badge) */}
                                <td className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <span 
                                        className={`inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${item.feature.type === FeatureType.QUANTITY ? 'bg-violet-900/40 text-accent-secondary' : 'bg-green-900/40 text-success'}`}
                                    >
                                        {item.feature.type === FeatureType.QUANTITY ? <FaCalculator className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" /> : <FaTag className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />}
                                        {item.feature.type === FeatureType.QUANTITY ? 'Quantitativo' : 'Booleano'}
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Campo de Edição (Valor / Acesso) */}
                                <td className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {item.feature.type === FeatureType.BOOLEAN ? (
                                        // Toggle para Booleano
                                        <div className="flex items-center">
                                            <input
                                                id={`bool-${item.feature.id}`}
                                                type="checkbox"
                                                checked={item.limitValue === 1}
                                                onChange={(e) => handleBooleanToggle(item.feature.id, e.target.checked)}
                                                className="h-4 w-4 text-accent-secondary border-gray-600 rounded"
                                            />
                                            <label htmlFor={`bool-${item.feature.id}`} className="ml-2 text-sm text-gray-300">
                                                Permitir Acesso
                                            </label>
                                        </div>
                                    ) : (
                                        // Campo de Entrada para Quantidade
                                        <input
                                            type="number"
                                            value={item.limitValue}
                                            onChange={(e) => handleLimitChange(item.feature.id, e.target.value)}
                                            min="-1"
                                            className="block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                                            placeholder="-1 (Ilimitado) ou Limite"
                                        />
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {/* Botão de Sincronização */}
            <button
                onClick={handleSyncRules}
                disabled={isSyncing}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-base-dark bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-500 transition disabled:bg-gray-500"
            >
                <FaSave className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                {isSyncing ? 'Sincronizando Regras...' : 'Salvar Regras do Plano'}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/admin/components/PlanTable.tsx =====

"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { usePlans } from '@/core/hooks/usePlans';
import { FaEdit, FaTrash, FaCheckCircle, FaTimesCircle, FaDollarSign, FaPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Plan } from '@/core/types/acl'; 
import { PlanModal } from '@/modules/admin/components/PlanModal';

/**
 * @component PlanTable
 * @description Tabela de visualização e gerenciamento de Planos de Assinatura.
 * Porquê: Centraliza a interface CRUD e a representação visual dos dados de ACL.
 */
export const PlanTable: React.FC = () => {
    
    // --- Definição do Estado do Modal ---
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [planToEdit, setPlanToEdit] = useState<Plan | undefined>(undefined);
    // -------------------------------------------------

    const { plans, isLoading, error, refreshData } = usePlans();
    // FUTURO: Integração do hook de mutação
    // const { deletePlan, isLoading: isDeleting } = usePlanMutation();
    const isDeleting = false; // Placeholder
    
    // Recarrega os dados após sucesso na mutação (FUTURO: Deve chamar refreshData do usePlans)
    const handleSuccess = () => {
        console.log("Plano alterado. Recarregando dados.");
        refreshData();
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        setPlanToEdit(undefined);
    };

    // --- Funções de Ação Futuras ---
    
    const handleNewPlan = () => {
        setPlanToEdit(undefined); // Criação de um novo plano (passa undefined)
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleEdit = (plan: Plan) => { // O tipo Plan é o importado de @/types/acl
        setPlanToEdit(plan);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };
    
    const handleDelete = (plan: Plan) => {
        if (confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja deletar o plano "${plan.name}"?`)) {
            console.log("Deletar o plano:", plan.name);
            // FUTURO: Chamar o Service de Delete no Backend.
        }
    };

    // --- Tratamento de Estado ---

    if (isLoading || isDeleting) {
        return (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-accent-secondary">
                <span className="animate-spin text-xl">⏳</span> Carregando Planos...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="p-4 bg-danger/20 border border-danger rounded-lg text-danger">
                {error}
            </div>
        );
    }
    
    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            
            {/* Botão de Criação */}
            <div className="flex justify-end">
                <button 
                    onClick={handleNewPlan}
                    className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 bg-accent-primary text-base-dark font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-orange-500 transition duration-150 shadow-lg"
                >
                    <FaPlus />
                    <span>Novo Plano</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Tabela Responsiva (Excelência) */}
            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-base-metallic rounded-lg shadow-xl">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-700">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Nome / Descrição
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Preço
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Status
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Ações
                            </th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                        {plans.map((plan) => (
                            <tr key={plan.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700 transition duration-100">
                                {/* Nome / Descrição */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div className="text-sm font-medium text-white">{plan.name}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 max-w-sm truncate">{plan.description || 'Nenhuma descrição fornecida.'}</div>
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Preço */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-green-400 font-bold">
                                    <span className="flex items-center">
                                        <FaDollarSign className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                                        {plan.price.toFixed(2)}
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Status */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center text-sm">
                                    {plan.isActive ? (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 bg-success/20 text-success rounded-full">
                                            <FaCheckCircle className="w-3 h-3" />
                                            <span>Ativo</span>
                                        </span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 bg-danger/20 text-danger rounded-full">
                                            <FaTimesCircle className="w-3 h-3" />
                                            <span>Inativo</span>
                                        </span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Ações (CRUD) */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center text-sm font-medium space-x-3">
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleEdit(plan)}
                                        className="text-accent-secondary hover:text-violet-400"
                                        title="Editar Plano"
                                    >
                                        <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleDelete(plan)}
                                        className="text-danger hover:text-red-600"
                                        title="Deletar Plano"
                                    >
                                        <FaTrash className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            
            {plans.length === 0 && !isLoading && !error && (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500">
                    Nenhum plano cadastrado. Comece criando o seu primeiro plano!
                </div>
            )}
            
            {/* --- MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO/EDIÇÃO (FUTURO) --- */}
            {/* O Modal precisa ser renderizado aqui para o estado funcionar */}
            {/* Exemplo de integração (assumindo que PlanModal foi importado e props definidos): */}
            <PlanModal
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                currentPlan={planToEdit}
                onSuccess={handleSuccess}
            />
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/admin/components/TenantModal.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaLink } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Tenant, TenantModalProps, UpdateTenantAdminDto } from '@/core/types/tenant';
import { Plan } from '@/core/types/acl';
import { useTenantAdminMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useTenantAdminMutation';
import { usePlans } from '@/core/hooks/usePlans';

// --- NOVO: Função de Auxílio para Renderizar as Opções de Plano ---
const renderPlanOptions = (plans: Plan[]) => {
    return plans.map((plan: Plan) => (
        <option 
            key={plan.id} 
            value={plan.id} 
            // Regra: Não permitir selecionar Planos inativos
            disabled={!plan.isActive}
        >
            {plan.name} {plan.isActive ? '' : '(Inativo)'}
        </option>
    ));
};

/**
 * @component TenantModal
 * @description Modal de Edição/Soft Delete de Tenants, usado pelo GLOBAL_ADMIN.
 */
export const TenantModal: React.FC<TenantModalProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    currentTenant, 
    onSuccess 
}) => {
    
    // 1. HOOKS DE DADOS E MUTATION
    const { updateTenant, toggleActiveStatus, isLoading: isMutating, error: mutationError } = useTenantAdminMutation();
    const { plans, isLoading: loadingPlans, error: plansError } = usePlans();
    
    // 2. ESTADO DO FORMULÁRIO
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState<UpdateTenantAdminDto>({
        razaoSocial: currentTenant.razaoSocial,
        nomeFantasia: currentTenant.nomeFantasia || '',
        planoId: currentTenant.planoId,
        isActive: currentTenant.isActive,
    });
    const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // Sincroniza o formulário ao abrir ou se o Tenant mudar
    useEffect(() => {
        setFormData({
            razaoSocial: currentTenant.razaoSocial,
            nomeFantasia: currentTenant.nomeFantasia || '',
            planoId: currentTenant.planoId,
            isActive: currentTenant.isActive,
        });
        setSubmitError(null);
    }, [currentTenant, isOpen]);

    // Lida com a mudança nos campos
    const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const { name, value, type } = e.target;
        
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            [name]: type === 'checkbox' 
                      ? (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked 
                      : value,
        }));
    };
    
    // 3. LÓGICA DE SUBMISSÃO (Edição)
    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSubmitError(null);

        try {
            // Apenas envia campos que foram modificados no formulário
            const changes: UpdateTenantAdminDto = {};
            if (formData.razaoSocial !== currentTenant.razaoSocial) changes.razaoSocial = formData.razaoSocial;
            if (formData.nomeFantasia !== currentTenant.nomeFantasia) changes.nomeFantasia = formData.nomeFantasia;
            if (formData.planoId !== currentTenant.planoId) changes.planoId = formData.planoId;
            // isActive é tratado pelo toggleStatus

            if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
                 await updateTenant(currentTenant.id, changes);
            }
            
            onSuccess(); // Chama a recarga da tabela
            onClose();   // Fecha o modal
            
        } catch (err: any) {
            setSubmitError(mutationError || err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao salvar edições.');
        }
    };
    
    // 4. LÓGICA DE SOFT DELETE/ATIVE
    const handleToggleStatus = async () => {
        try {
            await toggleActiveStatus(currentTenant.id, currentTenant.isActive);
            onSuccess();
        } catch (err) {
             setSubmitError(mutationError || 'Falha ao alterar o status do Tenant.');
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center">
            <div className="bg-base-metallic p-6 rounded-lg shadow-2xl w-full max-w-lg">
                
                {/* Cabeçalho do Modal */}
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-700 pb-3 mb-4">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">
                        Gerenciar Tenant: {currentTenant.razaoSocial}
                    </h2>
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white">
                        <FaTimes className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Mensagens de Erro e Loading */}
                {(submitError || mutationError || plansError) && (
                    <div className="p-3 mb-4 text-sm font-medium text-danger bg-red-900/50 rounded-md">
                        {submitError || mutationError || plansError}
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Status Atual e Ação Soft Delete */}
                <div className={`p-3 mb-4 rounded-lg flex justify-between items-center ${currentTenant.isActive ? 'bg-success/20 text-success' : 'bg-danger/20 text-danger'}`}>
                    <span className="font-semibold">
                        Status Atual: {currentTenant.isActive ? 'ATIVO' : 'INATIVO'}
                    </span>
                    <button 
                        onClick={handleToggleStatus}
                        disabled={isMutating || loadingPlans}
                        className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium rounded-lg border border-current hover:opacity-80 transition"
                    >
                        <FaLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                        <span>{currentTenant.isActive ? 'Desativar Conta' : 'Reativar Conta'}</span>
                    </button>
                </div>


                {/* Formulário de Edição */}
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                    
                    {/* Razão Social */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Razão Social</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="razaoSocial"
                            value={formData.razaoSocial}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            required
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                        />
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Nome Fantasia */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Nome Fantasia</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            name="nomeFantasia"
                            value={formData.nomeFantasia || ''}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white"
                        />
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Seleção de Plano */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300">Alterar Plano de Assinatura</label>
                        <select
                            name="planoId"
                            value={formData.planoId}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            disabled={loadingPlans || isMutating}
                            className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm bg-gray-700 text-white disabled:bg-gray-600"
                        >
                            {loadingPlans ? (
                                <option>Carregando Planos...</option>
                            ) : (
                                // --- CORREÇÃO: Chamada à função auxiliar ---
                                renderPlanOptions(plans) 
                                // ------------------------------------------
                            )}
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    {/* Botão de Ação */}
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={isMutating || loadingPlans}
                        className="w-full flex justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 transition disabled:bg-gray-500"
                    >
                        {isMutating ? 'Salvando...' : 'Salvar Detalhes do Tenant'}
                    </button>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/admin/components/TenantTable.tsx =====

"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaEdit, FaCheckCircle, FaTimesCircle, FaLink, FaBuilding, FaUserTie, FaPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useTenants } from '@/core/hooks/useTenants';
import { UserRole } from '@/core/types/user'; 
import { TenantWithPlan } from '@/core/types/tenant';
import { TenantModal } from './TenantModal';
import { useTenantAdminMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useTenantAdminMutation';
import { useNavigation } from '@/core/hooks/useNavigation';

/**
 * @component TenantTable
 * @description Tabela de visualização e gerenciamento de Tenants (Empresas).
 * Porquê: Permite ao GLOBAL_ADMIN visualizar e gerenciar o status e os planos de cada Tenant.
 */
export const TenantTable: React.FC = () => {
    
    // 1. ESTADO PARA MODAL E DADOS DE EDIÇÃO
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [tenantToEdit, setTenantToEdit] = useState<TenantWithPlan | undefined>(undefined);

    // Hooks de Dados e Mutação
    const { tenants, isLoading: loadingTenants, error, refreshData } = useTenants();
    // Assumindo refreshData para recarregar
    const { toggleActiveStatus, isLoading: isToggling } = useTenantAdminMutation();
    // --- NOVO: Hook de Roteamento ---
    // MODIFICAÇÃO: Usamos o hook useNavigation.
    const { toRegister } = useNavigation();
    // Recarrega os dados após sucesso na mutação
    const handleSuccess = () => {
        console.log("Tenant alterado. Recarregando dados.");
    // FUTURO: Chamar refreshData()
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        setTenantToEdit(undefined);
    };
    // --- Funções de Ação ---
    const handleCreateNewTenant = () => {
        // Redireciona o Admin Global para a tela de Registro/Criação de Conta.
        // MODIFICAÇÃO: Usa o método centralizado toRegister.
        toRegister();
    };

    const handleEdit = (tenant: TenantWithPlan) => {
        setTenantToEdit(tenant);
        // Define o tenant a ser editado
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };
    const handleSoftDelete = async (tenant: TenantWithPlan) => {
        const action = tenant.isActive ? 'desativar' : 'ativar';
        if (confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja ${action} o Tenant "${tenant.razaoSocial}"?`)) {
            try {
                // Chamada de Mutação Soft Delete
                await toggleActiveStatus(tenant.id, tenant.isActive);
                console.log(`Tenant ${tenant.razaoSocial} ${action} com sucesso.`);
                // FUTURO: Chamar refreshData()
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`Falha ao ${action} o Tenant:`, error);
            }
        }
    };
    // --- Helpers de Visualização ---
    const renderStatusBadge = (isActive: boolean) => {
        const colorClass = isActive ?'text-success bg-green-900/40' : 'text-danger bg-red-900/40';
        const icon = isActive ? <FaCheckCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaTimesCircle className="w-3 h-3" />;
        return (
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${colorClass}`}>
                {icon}
                <span>{isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}</span>
            </span>
        );
    };

    // A Role Badge não é usada para Tenants, mas o helper é mantido.
    const renderRoleBadge = (role: typeof UserRole[keyof typeof UserRole]) => {
        const colorClass = role === UserRole.GLOBAL_ADMIN ? 'text-accent-secondary bg-violet-900/40' : 'text-accent-primary bg-orange-900/40';
        const icon = role === UserRole.GLOBAL_ADMIN ? <FaUserTie className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaBuilding className="w-3 h-3" />;

        return (
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${colorClass}`}>
                {icon}
                <span>{role}</span>
            </span>
        );
    };

    // --- Tratamento de Estado ---
    if (loadingTenants || isToggling) {
        return (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-accent-secondary">
                <span className="animate-spin text-xl">⏳</span> {isToggling ? 'Alterando Status...' : 'Carregando Lista de Tenants...'}
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="p-4 bg-danger/20 border border-danger rounded-lg text-danger">
                {error}
            </div>
        );
    }
    
    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            
            {/* Botão de Criação (Placeholder - Criação de Tenant é pelo /register) */}
            <div className="flex justify-end">
                <button 
                    // NOTE: A criação de tenant é via /register. Mantemos o placeholder inativo.
                    onClick={handleCreateNewTenant} 
                    className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 bg-accent-primary text-base-dark font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-orange-500 transition duration-150 shadow-lg"                >
                    <FaPlus />
                    <span>Novo Tenant</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Tabela Responsiva */}
            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-base-metallic rounded-lg shadow-xl">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-700">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Razão Social / Documento
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                               Plano Atual
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Contato
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Status
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Ações
                            </th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                        {tenants.map((tenant) => (
                            <tr key={tenant.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700 transition duration-100">
                                {/* Nome / Documento */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div className="text-sm font-medium text-white">{tenant.razaoSocial}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">CNPJ/CPF: {tenant.cnpjCpf}</div>
                                </td>
                           
                                  {/* Plano Atual */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-accent-secondary">
                                    {tenant.plan ? tenant.plan.name : 'N/A'}
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Contato */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-200">
                                    {tenant.emailContato}
                                </td>
                                
                                {/* Status */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center">
                                    {renderStatusBadge(tenant.isActive)}
                                </td>
        
                                {/* Ações (CRUD) */}
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center text-sm font-medium space-x-3">
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleEdit(tenant)}
                                        className="text-accent-primary hover:text-orange-500"
                                        title="Editar Plano e Dados"
                                    >
                                    <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleSoftDelete(tenant)}
                                        disabled={isToggling}
                                        className={tenant.isActive ? 'text-danger hover:text-red-600' : 'text-success hover:text-green-500'}
                                        title={tenant.isActive ? 'Desativar Tenant' : 'Ativar Tenant'}
                                    >
                                        <FaLink className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            
            {tenants.length === 0 && !loadingTenants && !error && (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500">
                    Nenhum Tenant cadastrado além do ambiente de teste.
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO/EDIÇÃO --- */}
            {isModalOpen && tenantToEdit && (
                <TenantModal
                    isOpen={isModalOpen}
                    onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                    currentTenant={tenantToEdit}
                    onSuccess={handleSuccess}
                />
            )}
            {/* ------------------------------- */}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/components/CategoryForm.tsx =====

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { FaSave, FaArrowLeft } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Imports do Módulo
import { useCategories } from '../hooks/useCategories';
import { useCategoryMutation } from '../hooks/useCategoryMutation';
import { ICreateCategoryDTO } from '../types';

/**
 * Formulário de Criação/Edição de Categorias.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Gerenciar o estado local dos campos (Nome, Pai).
 * - Carregar a lista de categorias para o Select de "Pai".
 * - Executar a validação básica e submissão via Mutation.
 */
export function CategoryForm() {
    // 1. Contexto de Navegação
    const router = useRouter();
    const params = useParams();
    const tenantId = params.tenantId as string;

    // 2. Hooks de Dados
    // Precisamos das categorias existentes para preencher o Select de "Categoria Pai"
    const { categories, isLoading: isLoadingList, refreshCategories } = useCategories();
    const { createCategory, isLoading: isSaving, error: mutationError } = useCategoryMutation();

    // 3. Estado Local do Formulário
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState<ICreateCategoryDTO>({
        name: '',
        parentId: '' // String vazia indica "Sem Pai" (Raiz)
    });

    // Carrega a lista ao montar para garantir que o select de pais esteja atualizado
    useEffect(() => {
        refreshCategories();
    }, [refreshCategories]);

    // 4. Handlers
    const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        if (!formData.name.trim()) {
            alert('O nome da categoria é obrigatório.');
            return;
        }

        // Prepara o payload (converte string vazia de parentId para undefined)
        const payload: ICreateCategoryDTO = {
            name: formData.name,
            parentId: formData.parentId === '' ? undefined : formData.parentId
        };

        const result = await createCategory(payload);

        // Se sucesso (retornou objeto), redireciona
        if (result) {
            router.push(`/${tenantId}/dashboard/categories`);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6 p-6 animate-fade-in">
            {/* Header com Navegação */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <Link 
                        href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/categories`}
                        className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-800 text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors"
                    >
                        <FaArrowLeft />
                    </Link>
                    <div>
                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Nova Categoria</h1>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Preencha os dados para criar um novo departamento</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Tratamento de Erro (API) */}
            {mutationError && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-900/20 border border-red-500/50 text-red-200 rounded-lg text-sm">
                    ⚠️ {mutationError}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Container do Formulário */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-base-metallic p-6 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 shadow-lg space-y-6">
                
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                    {/* Campo: Nome */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label htmlFor="name" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400">
                            Nome da Categoria <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            id="name"
                            value={formData.name}
                            onChange={(e) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, name: e.target.value }))}
                            placeholder="Ex: Bebidas, Lanches..."
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:border-transparent outline-none transition-all"
                            disabled={isSaving}
                            autoFocus
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Campo: Categoria Pai */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label htmlFor="parentId" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400">
                            Categoria Pai (Opcional)
                        </label>
                        <select
                            id="parentId"
                            value={formData.parentId || ''}
                            onChange={(e) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, parentId: e.target.value }))}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:border-transparent outline-none transition-all appearance-none"
                            disabled={isSaving || isLoadingList}
                        >
                            <option value="">Nenhuma (Categoria Raiz)</option>
                            {categories.map((cat) => (
                                <option key={cat.id} value={cat.id}>
                                    {cat.name}
                                </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                            Selecione apenas se esta for uma subcategoria.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Footer com Ações */}
                <div className="pt-4 border-t border-gray-700 flex justify-end gap-3">
                    <Link href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/categories`}>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800 hover:text-white transition-colors font-medium"
                            disabled={isSaving}
                        >
                            Cancelar
                        </button>
                    </Link>
                    
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={isSaving}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-accent-primary/90 text-base-dark font-bold py-2 px-6 rounded-lg transition-all shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    >
                        {isSaving ? (
                            <>
                                <span className="animate-spin">⏳</span> Salvando...
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <FaSave /> Salvar Categoria
                            </>
                        )}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/components/CategoryList.tsx =====

'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { FaPlus, FaFolder } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Hooks e Componentes Internos
import { useCategories } from '../hooks/useCategories';
import { useCategoryMutation } from '../hooks/useCategoryMutation';
import { CategoryListGrid } from './CategoryListGrid';

/**
 * Container Principal da Listagem de Categorias.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Orquestrar a lógica da tela (Hooks, Estados).
 * - Renderizar o Cabeçalho e Botões de Ação.
 * - Alimentar o Grid com dados.
 */
export function CategoryList() {
    const params = useParams();
    const tenantId = params.tenantId as string;

    // Lógica de Negócio
    const { categories, isLoading, error, refreshCategories } = useCategories();
    const { deleteCategory } = useCategoryMutation();

    useEffect(() => {
        refreshCategories();
    }, [refreshCategories]);

    const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
        if (!window.confirm('Tem certeza que deseja remover esta categoria?')) return;
        const success = await deleteCategory(id);
        if (success) {
            await refreshCategories();
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="w-full space-y-6 p-6 animate-fade-in">
            {/* Header / Actions Area */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 bg-base-metallic p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 shadow-lg">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="p-3 bg-gray-800 rounded-lg text-accent-primary border border-gray-700">
                        <FaFolder className="text-xl" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                            Categorias
                        </h1>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                            Gestão de departamentos e hierarquia
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <Link href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/products/categories/new`}>
                    <button className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-accent-primary/90 text-base-dark font-bold py-2.5 px-5 rounded-md transition-all shadow-md hover:shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-0.5">
                        <FaPlus />
                        <span>Nova Categoria</span>
                    </button>
                </Link>
            </div>

            {/* Tratamento de Erro */}
            {error && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-900/20 border border-red-500/50 text-red-200 rounded-lg text-sm">
                    ⚠️ {error}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Grid de Dados (Componente Puro) */}
            <CategoryListGrid
                categories={categories}
                isLoading={isLoading}
                onDelete={handleDelete}
                tenantId={tenantId}
            />
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/components/CategoryListGrid.tsx =====

import Link from 'next/link';
import { FaEdit, FaTrash } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { ICategory } from '../types';

/**
 * Interface de Props do Componente.
 * Segue o padrão "Presentational Component": recebe dados e funções do pai.
 * Não faz chamadas de API diretamente.
 */
interface CategoryListGridProps {
    categories: ICategory[];
    isLoading: boolean;
    onDelete: (id: string) => void;
    tenantId: string;
}

/**
 * Componente de Grid de Listagem de Categorias.
 * * Padrão Visual:
 * - Tabela com background bg-gray-800/50
 * - Bordas sutis border-gray-700
 * - Hover nas linhas para feedback visual
 * - Ícones via react-icons/fa
 */
export function CategoryListGrid({ 
    categories, 
    isLoading, 
    onDelete, 
    tenantId 
}: CategoryListGridProps) {
    
    return (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 shadow-sm">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-gray-400">
                {/* Cabeçalho Padronizado */}
                <thead className="bg-gray-900/50 text-xs uppercase text-gray-400 font-semibold">
                    <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Nome da Categoria</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Categoria Pai</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>

                {/* Corpo da Tabela */}
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700/50">
                    {/* Estado de Carregamento */}
                    {isLoading && (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan={3} className="px-6 py-8 text-center text-gray-500 animate-pulse">
                                Carregando dados...
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    )}

                    {/* Estado Vazio */}
                    {!isLoading && categories.length === 0 && (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan={3} className="px-6 py-8 text-center text-gray-500 italic">
                                Nenhuma categoria encontrada.
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    )}

                    {/* Listagem de Dados */}
                    {!isLoading && categories.map((category) => (
                        <tr 
                            key={category.id} 
                            className="hover:bg-gray-700/50 transition-colors duration-200"
                        >
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-gray-200">
                                {category.name}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                {category.parentId ? (
                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-gray-700 text-blue-400 border border-blue-900/30">
                                        Subcategoria
                                    </span>
                                ) : (
                                    <span className="text-gray-600">-</span>
                                )}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                                <div className="flex justify-end items-center gap-3">
                                    {/* Botão Editar: Navega para a rota de edição */}
                                    <Link 
                                        href={`/${tenantId}/dashboard/products/categories/${category.id}`}
                                        className="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors p-1"
                                        title="Editar Categoria"
                                    >
                                        <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </Link>

                                    {/* Botão Excluir: Dispara a prop onDelete */}
                                    <button
                                        onClick={() => onDelete(category.id)}
                                        className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 transition-colors p-1 hover:bg-red-900/20 rounded"
                                        title="Excluir Categoria"
                                    >
                                        <FaTrash className="w-4 h-4" />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    ))}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/hooks/useCategories.ts =====

import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { ICategory } from '../types';

/**
 * Hook de Leitura (Query) para o Módulo de Categorias.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Buscar e armazenar a lista de categorias.
 * - Expor estado de carregamento e erro para a UI.
 */
export function useCategories() {
    const [categories, setCategories] = useState<ICategory[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const fetchCategories = useCallback(async () => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            const response = await api.get<ICategory[]>('/categories');
            setCategories(response.data);
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao buscar categorias:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Erro ao carregar lista de categorias.';
            setError(msg);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    // Busca inicial automática
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchCategories();
    }, [fetchCategories]);

    return {
        categories,
        isLoading,
        error,
        refreshCategories: fetchCategories
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/hooks/useCategoryMutation.ts =====

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { ICategory, ICreateCategoryDTO, IUpdateCategoryDTO } from '../types';

/**
 * Hook de Mutação para o Módulo de Categorias.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Gerenciar operações de escrita (C/U/D).
 * - Centralizar tratamento de erro via estado local (Padrão do Projeto).
 */
export function useCategoryMutation() {
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const router = useRouter();

    /**
     * Cria uma nova categoria.
     */
    const createCategory = async (data: ICreateCategoryDTO): Promise<ICategory | null> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            const response = await api.post<ICategory>('/categories', data);
            
            // O sucesso é tratado pelo componente (ex: redirecionar ou fechar modal)
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao criar categoria:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao criar a categoria.';
            setError(msg);
            throw err; // Re-lança para que o form possa reagir se necessário
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Atualiza uma categoria existente.
     */
    const updateCategory = async (id: string, data: IUpdateCategoryDTO): Promise<ICategory | null> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            const response = await api.put<ICategory>(`/categories/${id}`, data);
            router.refresh();
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao atualizar categoria:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao atualizar a categoria.';
            setError(msg);
            throw err;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Remove uma categoria.
     */
    const deleteCategory = async (id: string): Promise<boolean> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            await api.delete(`/categories/${id}`);
            return true;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao remover categoria:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Não foi possível remover a categoria. Verifique se há vínculos.';
            setError(msg);
            return false;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        createCategory,
        updateCategory,
        deleteCategory,
        isLoading,
        error // Retornado para exibição no componente
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/types.ts =====

export type AttributeType = 'text' | 'number' | 'select' | 'boolean';

export interface IAttributeSchema {
  key: string;       // Identificador único do campo (ex: "voltagem")
  label: string;     // Nome visível (ex: "Voltagem")
  type: AttributeType;
  required: boolean;
  options?: string[]; // Para selects (ex: ["110v", "220v"])
}

/**
 * Definição de Tipos para o Módulo de Categorias.
 * Espelha as entidades do Backend para garantir integridade na comunicação.
 */

export interface ICategory {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    parentId?: string | null;
    tenantId: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    isActive: boolean;
    // Opcional: Para visualização em árvore
    children?: ICategory[];
}

export interface ICreateCategoryDTO {
    name: string;
    parentId?: string;
}

export interface IUpdateCategoryDTO extends Partial<ICreateCategoryDTO> {}

/**
 * Definição de Tipos para o Módulo de Categorias.
 * * Espelha as entidades do Backend para garantir integridade na comunicação.
 */

export interface Category {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    parentId?: string | null;
    tenantId: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    isActive: boolean;
    // Opcional: Se o backend retornar a árvore montada
    children?: Category[]; 
}

export interface CreateCategoryDto {
    name: string;
    parentId?: string;
    isActive: boolean;
}

export interface UpdateCategoryDto extends Partial<CreateCategoryDto> {}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/AnalysisActionCard.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/AnalysisActionCard.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaUser, FaEnvelope, FaPhone, FaBullseye, FaSave, FaLink, 
    FaCheckCircle, FaExclamationTriangle, FaPen, FaMapMarkerAlt 
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { InteractionAnalysis } from '../types';
import { Client, AddressType } from '@/core/types/client';
import { useInteractionMutation, ClientActionType } from '../hooks/useInteractionMutation';

interface AnalysisActionCardProps {
    analysis: InteractionAnalysis;
    content: string;
    contextClient: Client | null;
    onSuccess: () => void;
}

export const AnalysisActionCard: React.FC<AnalysisActionCardProps> = ({
    analysis,
    content,
    contextClient,
    onSuccess
}) => {
    const { registerInteraction, isLoading, error, success } = useInteractionMutation();
    
    // Identidade extraída (atalho)
    const identity = analysis.extractedIdentity || {
        personName: '',
        companyName: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        extractedAddresses: [],
        intentType: 'UNKNOWN'
    };

    // --- ESTADO DO FORMULÁRIO ---
    // Agora inclui dados de endereço aninhados
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        name: '',
        document: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        // Dados de Endereço (Focamos no endereço principal para este card rápido)
        address: {
            street: '',
            number: '',
            district: '',
            city: '',
            state: '',
            zipCode: ''
        }
    });

    // Sincroniza o form quando a análise muda
    useEffect(() => {
        // Tenta pegar o primeiro endereço extraído pela IA
        const detectedAddress = identity.extractedAddresses?.[0];

        setFormData({
            name: identity.personName || 'Novo Lead',
            document: '', 
            email: identity.email || '',
            phone: identity.phone || '',
            address: {
                street: detectedAddress?.street || '',
                number: detectedAddress?.number || '',
                district: detectedAddress?.district || '',
                city: detectedAddress?.city || '',
                state: detectedAddress?.state || '',
                zipCode: detectedAddress?.zipCode || ''
            }
        });
    }, [analysis]); 

    const [mode, setMode] = useState<'VIEW' | 'CREATE_EDIT'>('VIEW');

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
    };

    // Handler específico para campos de endereço aninhados
    const handleAddressChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        setFormData({
            ...formData,
            address: {
                ...formData.address,
                [e.target.name]: e.target.value
            }
        });
    };

    const handleLinkExisting = async () => {
        if (!contextClient) return;
        await registerInteraction({
            action: ClientActionType.LINK_EXISTING,
            clientId: contextClient.id,
            type: 'NOTE', 
            content: content,
            aiAnalysis: analysis
        });
        if (!error) onSuccess();
    };

    const handleCreateNew = async () => {
        if (!formData.name.trim()) {
            alert("O Nome do cliente é obrigatório.");
            return;
        }

        // Monta o array de endereços apenas se houver dados mínimos (Rua)
        const addressesToSend = formData.address.street.trim() ? [{
            street: formData.address.street,
            number: formData.address.number || 'S/N', // Fallback seguro
            district: formData.address.district || 'Centro',
            city: formData.address.city || 'Indefinida',
            state: formData.address.state || 'UF',
            zipCode: formData.address.zipCode || '00000-000',
            type: AddressType.MAIN
        }] : undefined;

        await registerInteraction({
            action: ClientActionType.CREATE_NEW,
            type: 'NOTE',
            content: content,
            aiAnalysis: analysis,
            newClientData: {
                name: formData.name,
                document: formData.document.trim() || undefined, 
                email: formData.email.trim() || undefined,
                phone: formData.phone.trim() || undefined,
                addresses: addressesToSend
            }
        });

        if (!error) onSuccess();
    };

    if (success) {
        return (
            <div className="bg-green-900/20 border border-green-500/50 p-6 rounded-xl text-center animate-fade-in">
                <FaCheckCircle className="w-12 h-12 text-green-500 mx-auto mb-3" />
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Registrado com Sucesso!</h3>
                <button onClick={onSuccess} className="mt-4 text-sm text-green-400 hover:underline">Continuar</button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // Helper para exibir endereço formatado
    const formattedAddress = formData.address.street 
        ? `${formData.address.street}, ${formData.address.number || 'S/N'} - ${formData.address.city}/${formData.address.state}`
        : null;

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-800 border border-gray-600 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden transition-all">
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-1 h-full bg-accent-secondary"></div>
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 pl-2">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                        <FaUser className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> 
                        {mode === 'VIEW' ? 'Dados Detectados (IA)' : 'Revisão de Cadastro'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                        {mode === 'VIEW' ? 'Verifique os dados encontrados.' : 'Edite os dados antes de salvar.'}
                    </p>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] text-accent-primary font-bold border border-gray-600 uppercase">
                    <FaBullseye className="mr-1 inline" /> {identity.intentType}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- MODO VISUALIZAÇÃO (READ-ONLY) --- */}
            {mode === 'VIEW' && (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm mb-4 pl-2">
                    <div className="bg-gray-900 p-2 rounded border border-gray-700 col-span-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 block uppercase font-bold">Nome</span>
                        <span className="text-white font-medium">{identity.personName || '-'}</span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="bg-gray-900 p-2 rounded border border-gray-700">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 block uppercase font-bold">E-mail</span>
                        <div className="flex items-center text-white mt-1">
                            <FaEnvelope className="mr-2 text-gray-600" />
                            <span className="truncate text-xs">{identity.email || '-'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-gray-900 p-2 rounded border border-gray-700">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 block uppercase font-bold">Telefone</span>
                        <div className="flex items-center text-white mt-1">
                            <FaPhone className="mr-2 text-gray-600" />
                            <span className="truncate text-xs">{identity.phone || '-'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Exibição de Endereço Detectado */}
                    {formattedAddress && (
                        <div className="bg-gray-900 p-2 rounded border border-gray-700 col-span-2">
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 block uppercase font-bold">Endereço Sugerido</span>
                            <div className="flex items-center text-white mt-1">
                                <FaMapMarkerAlt className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" />
                                <span className="truncate text-xs">{formattedAddress}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODO EDIÇÃO (FORMULÁRIO COMPLETO) --- */}
            {mode === 'CREATE_EDIT' && (
                <div className="space-y-3 mb-4 pl-2 animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Dados Básicos */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-accent-primary font-bold uppercase block mb-1">Nome *</label>
                        <input type="text" name="name" value={formData.name} onChange={handleChange}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase block mb-1">E-mail</label>
                            <input type="email" name="email" value={formData.email} onChange={handleChange}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase block mb-1">Telefone</label>
                            <input type="text" name="phone" value={formData.phone} onChange={handleChange}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Seção de Endereço */}
                    <div className="border-t border-gray-700 pt-3 mt-2">
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-300 font-bold uppercase block mb-2 flex items-center">
                            <FaMapMarkerAlt className="mr-1" /> Endereço Principal
                        </label>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                            <div className="col-span-3">
                                <input type="text" name="street" placeholder="Rua / Logradouro" value={formData.address.street} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <input type="text" name="number" placeholder="Nº" value={formData.address.number} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <input type="text" name="district" placeholder="Bairro" value={formData.address.district} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <input type="text" name="city" placeholder="Cidade" value={formData.address.city} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-1">
                                <input type="text" name="state" placeholder="UF" maxLength={2} value={formData.address.state} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none uppercase" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-3">
                                <input type="text" name="zipCode" placeholder="CEP" value={formData.address.zipCode} onChange={handleAddressChange}
                                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase block mb-1">CPF / CNPJ (Opcional)</label>
                        <input type="text" name="document" value={formData.document} onChange={handleChange}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none" />
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Mensagem de Erro */}
            {error && (
                <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-900/40 border border-red-500/50 rounded flex items-center text-xs text-red-200">
                    <FaExclamationTriangle className="mr-2 flex-shrink-0" />
                    {error}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Ações */}
            <div className="pl-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-700">
                {mode === 'VIEW' ? (
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                        {contextClient ? (
                            <button onClick={handleLinkExisting} disabled={isLoading} className="flex-1 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border border-gray-500">
                                {isLoading ? 'Vinculando...' : <><FaLink className="mr-2 inline" /> Vincular Existente</>}
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="flex-1 text-[10px] text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900/50 rounded border border-dashed border-gray-700 select-none">
                                Selecione na lista p/ vincular
                            </div>
                        )}
                        <button onClick={() => setMode('CREATE_EDIT')} className="flex-1 bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition shadow-lg">
                            <FaPen className="mr-2 inline" /> Editar e Salvar
                        </button>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                        <button onClick={() => setMode('VIEW')} className="flex-1 bg-gray-700 text-gray-300 py-2 rounded text-xs font-bold hover:bg-gray-600">Cancelar</button>
                        <button onClick={handleCreateNew} disabled={isLoading} className="flex-1 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-600 text-base-dark py-2 rounded text-xs font-bold shadow-lg">
                            {isLoading ? 'Salvando...' : 'Confirmar Lead'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/ai-widget/AIInputWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/ai-widget/AIInputWidget.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaMicrophone, FaPaperPlane, FaMagic, FaBrain } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface AIInputWidgetProps {
    onAnalyze: (text: string) => void;
    isAnalyzing: boolean;
    // --- NOVAS PROPS PARA CONTROLE DE ESTADO ---
    value: string;
    onChange: (text: string) => void;
}

/**
 * @component AIInputWidget
 * @description Console de entrada multimodal (Texto/Voz).
 * @update Refatorado para ser um componente CONTROLADO (Controlled Component).
 * O estado do texto agora reside no pai (CRMPage), permitindo limpeza externa.
 */
export const AIInputWidget: React.FC<AIInputWidgetProps> = ({ 
    onAnalyze, 
    isAnalyzing,
    value,      // Recebe do pai
    onChange    // Notifica o pai
}) => {
    
    // O estado local 'text' foi removido. Usamos 'value'.
    const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);

    const handleSend = () => {
        if (!value.trim()) return;
        onAnalyze(value);
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
            handleSend();
        }
    };

    const handleMicClick = () => {
        setIsRecording(!isRecording);
        if (!isRecording) {
            console.log("Microfone ativado...");
            // Futuro: Implementar Web Speech API aqui e chamar onChange(transcrição)
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="bg-base-metallic border-t border-gray-700 p-3 flex flex-col h-full">
            
            {/* Header do Widget */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <FaBrain className="text-accent-primary w-4 h-4" />
                    <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                        Filix IA
                    </label>
                </div>
                
                {isAnalyzing && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-accent-secondary flex items-center animate-pulse font-bold">
