                        <FaMagic className="mr-1" /> Processando...
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 relative">
                <textarea
                    value={value} // Controlado pelo Pai
                    onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} // Atualiza o Pai
                    onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
                    className="w-full h-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm text-white resize-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-700 placeholder-gray-500"
                    placeholder="Digite uma nota, cole um e-mail ou use o microfone..."
                    disabled={isAnalyzing}
                />
                
                <button 
                    onClick={handleMicClick}
                    className={`absolute bottom-2 right-2 p-2 rounded-full transition ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse' : 'bg-gray-800 text-gray-400 hover:text-white border border-gray-700 hover:border-gray-500'}`}
                    title="Gravar Áudio"
                >
                    <FaMicrophone className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-2">
                <button
                    onClick={handleSend}
                    disabled={isAnalyzing || !value.trim()}
                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-center py-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-600 text-base-dark font-bold rounded-lg transition disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-xs shadow-lg transform active:scale-95"
                >
                    {isAnalyzing ? 'Analisando...' : <><FaPaperPlane className="mr-2" /> Enviar para Análise</>}
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/ClientDashboard.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/ClientDashboard.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaUser, FaEdit, FaEnvelope, FaPhone, FaMapMarkerAlt, 
    FaIdCard, FaBrain, FaSave, FaTimes, 
    FaMagic, FaPlusCircle, FaStickyNote, 
    FaChevronDown, FaChevronUp
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Client, AddressType } from '@/core/types/client';
import { ClientForm } from '@/core/components/client/ClientForm';
import { InteractionTimeline } from './InteractionTimeline';

export type DashboardMode = 'VIEW' | 'EDIT' | 'CREATE';

interface ClientDashboardProps {
    client: Client | null;
    mode: DashboardMode;
    onModeChange: (mode: DashboardMode) => void;
    onUpdateSuccess: () => void;
}

type DashboardTab = 'OVERVIEW' | 'TIMELINE';

// --- SUB-COMPONENTE REUTILIZÁVEL: CARD EXPANSÍVEL ---
// Garante que IA e Notas tenham exatamente o mesmo comportamento e visual
const ExpandableInfoCard: React.FC<{ 
    title: string; 
    icon: React.ElementType; 
    content: string | null; 
    placeholder: string;
}> = ({ title, icon: Icon, content, placeholder }) => {
    
    const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
    // Define um limite de caracteres para ativar o collapse (ex: 200 chars)
    const shouldCollapse = content && content.length > 200;

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-800/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-600 p-4 relative overflow-hidden group transition-all hover:border-gray-500">
            {/* Barra lateral gradiente (Estilo unificado) */}
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-1 h-full bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-secondary to-accent-primary"></div>
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <Icon className="mr-2 text-accent-primary" /> {title}
                </h3>
            </div>
            
            <div className="relative">
                <div className={`text-sm text-gray-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${!isExpanded && shouldCollapse ? 'line-clamp-3' : ''}`}>
                    {content || <span className="text-gray-500 italic">{placeholder}</span>}
                </div>

                {/* Botão de Toggle (Só aparece se o texto for longo) */}
                {shouldCollapse && (
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
                        className="mt-2 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-accent-secondary hover:text-white flex items-center transition focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        {isExpanded ? (
                            <>Recolher <FaChevronUp className="ml-1" /></>
                        ) : (
                            <>Ler mais <FaChevronDown className="ml-1" /></>
                        )}
                    </button>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

/**
 * @component ClientDashboard
 * @description Painel Central Unificado.
 * @update Adicionado suporte a Collapse/Expand e novo card de Notas do Usuário.
 */
export const ClientDashboard: React.FC<ClientDashboardProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    mode, 
    onModeChange, 
    onUpdateSuccess 
}) => {
    
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<DashboardTab>('OVERVIEW');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (mode !== 'CREATE') {
            setActiveTab('OVERVIEW');
        }
    }, [client?.id, mode]);

    if (!client && mode !== 'CREATE') {
        return (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 bg-base-metallic/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50 border-dashed">
                <div className="p-4 bg-gray-800 rounded-full mb-4">
                    <FaUser className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-400">Nenhum Cliente Selecionado</h3>
                <p className="text-sm mt-2 max-w-xs text-center opacity-70">
                    Selecione um cliente na lista à esquerda ou clique em "Novo" para cadastrar.
                </p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const isCreating = mode === 'CREATE';
    const isFormOpen = mode === 'CREATE' || mode === 'EDIT';

    const displayClient = client || {
        name: 'Novo Cliente',
        document: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        isActive: true,
        aiSummary: null,
        userNotes: null, // Placeholder para criação
        addresses: []
    } as unknown as Client;

    const mainAddress = displayClient.addresses?.find(a => a.type === AddressType.MAIN) || displayClient.addresses?.[0];
    const addressText = mainAddress 
        ? `${mainAddress.street}, ${mainAddress.number} - ${mainAddress.district}, ${mainAddress.city}/${mainAddress.state}`
        : 'Sem endereço cadastrado';

    const handleFormSuccess = () => {
        onUpdateSuccess();
    };

    return (
        <div className="bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden shadow-2xl relative">
            
            {/* --- HEADER --- */}
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-800 to-gray-900 flex-shrink-0">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
                        <div className={`h-16 w-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 shadow-lg ${isCreating ? 'bg-gray-700 border-gray-500 text-gray-400' : 'bg-accent-primary/20 border-accent-primary text-accent-primary'}`}>
                            {isCreating ? (
                                <FaPlusCircle className="text-2xl" />
                            ) : (
                                <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{displayClient.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        
                        <div>
                            <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white tracking-tight">
                                {isCreating ? 'Cadastrando Novo Cliente' : displayClient.name}
                            </h1>
                            
                            {!isCreating && (
                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mt-1 text-sm text-gray-400">
                                    {displayClient.document ? (
                                        <span className="flex items-center"><FaIdCard className="mr-1.5 opacity-70" /> {displayClient.document}</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="flex items-center text-accent-secondary"><FaMagic className="mr-1.5" /> Lead (Em prospecção)</span>
                                    )}
                                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase border ${displayClient.isActive ? 'border-green-500/30 text-green-400 bg-green-500/10' : 'border-red-500/30 text-red-400 bg-red-500/10'}`}>
                                        {displayClient.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex space-x-2">
                        {isFormOpen ? (
                            <>
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => onModeChange('VIEW')}
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-gray-300 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold border border-gray-600"
                                >
                                    <FaTimes className="mr-1" /> Cancelar
                                </button>
                                <button 
                                    type="submit"
                                    form="client-edit-form"
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-white bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold shadow-lg"
                                >
                                    <FaSave className="mr-1" /> {isCreating ? 'Cadastrar' : 'Salvar'}
                                </button>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => onModeChange('EDIT')}
                                className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white bg-gray-700/50 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-lg transition border border-gray-600"
                                title="Editar Dados"
                            >
                                <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- NAVEGAÇÃO --- */}
            {!isFormOpen && (
                <div className="flex border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('OVERVIEW')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Visão Geral & IA
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('TIMELINE')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'TIMELINE' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Histórico de Interações
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL --- */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 custom-scrollbar">
                
                {isFormOpen ? (
                    // --- MODO FORMULÁRIO ---
                    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
                        
                        {/* Exibição dos Cards em modo "Read-Only/Disabled" visual durante a criação para manter layout */}
                        <div className="opacity-60 grayscale cursor-not-allowed">
                            <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                icon={FaBrain}
                                content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                placeholder="O dossiê será gerado automaticamente."
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-700">
                            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-accent-secondary uppercase mb-4">
                                {isCreating ? 'Preencha os dados do novo cliente' : 'Editando Dados Cadastrais'}
                            </h4>
                            <ClientForm 
                                client={isCreating ? undefined : client!} 
                                onSuccess={handleFormSuccess}
                                showSubmitButton={false}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    // --- MODO VISUALIZAÇÃO ---
                    <>
                        {activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' && (
                            <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
                                
                                {/* 1. Dossiê da IA */}
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                    icon={FaBrain}
                                    content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                    placeholder="Ainda não há um resumo gerado pela Inteligência Artificial."
                                />

                                {/* 2. Notas do Usuário (NOVO) */}
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Notas Pessoais"
                                    icon={FaStickyNote}
                                    content={displayClient.userNotes}
                                    placeholder="Nenhuma nota privada adicionada. Clique em editar para adicionar."
                                />

                                {/* 3. Dados Cadastrais */}
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    {/* Contato */}
                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Contato</h4>
                                        <div className="space-y-3">
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaEnvelope /></div>
                                                <span className="truncate">{displayClient.email || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaPhone /></div>
                                                <span>{displayClient.phone || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    {/* Endereço */}
                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Localização Principal</h4>
                                        <div className="flex items-start text-sm text-gray-200">
                                            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0"><FaMapMarkerAlt /></div>
                                            <span className="leading-relaxed">{addressText}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {activeTab === 'TIMELINE' && (
                            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-500 space-y-4">
                                <InteractionTimeline clientId={displayClient.id} />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/ClientInteractionManager.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa'; // Ícone para erro

// 1. Importa a Lógica (Hook)
import { useClientTimeline } from '../../hooks/useClientTimeline';

// 2. Importa a Visualização (Timeline)
import { InteractionTimeline } from './InteractionTimeline';

// 3. Importa a Entrada Unificada (que já criamos)
import { AgentInput } from '@/core/ai/components/AgentInput';

// 4. Importa Tipos necessários
import { InteractionType } from '../../types';

interface ClientInteractionManagerProps {
    clientId: string;
}

export const ClientInteractionManager: React.FC<ClientInteractionManagerProps> = ({ clientId }) => {
    // Inicializa o Hook
    const { 
        interactions, 
        loading, 
        analyzing, 
        error, 
        fetchTimeline, 
        registerInteraction 
    } = useClientTimeline(clientId);

    // Carrega os dados ao montar o componente
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchTimeline();
    }, [fetchTimeline]);

    /**
     * Handler para quando o usuário envia algo pelo AgentInput.
     * Aqui fazemos a ponte entre o componente genérico de Input e a lógica específica do CRM.
     */
    const handleAgentSend = async (text: string, attachments?: File[]) => {
        // Por padrão, enviamos como NOTE.
        // O Backend/IA que se vire para reclassificar se for um 'Compromisso' ou 'Ligação'.
        // Isso simplifica a vida do usuário (ele só digita).
        await registerInteraction(text, InteractionType.NOTE);
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full space-y-6">
            
            {/* --- ÁREA DE INPUT (O AGENTE) --- */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800/30 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-3 ml-1">
                    Novo Registro / Comando para IA
                </h3>
                
                {/* Reusando o componente AgentInput no modo 'compose' (bloco de notas) */}
                <AgentInput 
                    mode="compose"
                    placeholder="Ex: Liguei para o cliente e ele pediu para retornar terça..."
                    onSend={handleAgentSend}
                    isLoading={analyzing} // Trava o input enquanto a IA processa
                />
                
                {/* Feedback visual se a IA estiver pensando */}
                {analyzing && (
                    <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-accent-primary animate-pulse ml-1">
                        ✨ A IA está analisando sua nota e atualizando o cliente...
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* --- TRATAMENTO DE ERRO --- */}
            {error && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-900/20 border border-red-500/30 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-3 text-red-400">
                    <FaExclamationTriangle />
                    <span className="text-sm">{error}</span>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- LINHA DO TEMPO --- */}
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-0">
                <InteractionTimeline 
                    interactions={interactions} 
                    loading={loading} 
                />
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-dashboard/InteractionTimeline.tsx =====

import React from 'react';
// Importação dos ícones do pacote padrão do projeto (react-icons/fa)
import { 
    FaPhone,        // Para Ligações
    FaEnvelope,     // Para Emails
    FaWhatsapp,     // Para conversas de Zap
    FaStickyNote,   // Para notas gerais/padrão
    FaCalendarAlt,  // Para reuniões/agendamentos
    FaRobot,        // Badge para indicar conteúdo gerado por IA
    FaUser          // Fallback genérico
} from 'react-icons/fa'; 

// Importamos os tipos que definimos anteriormente para garantir tipagem forte
import { CRMInteraction, InteractionType } from '../../types';

interface InteractionTimelineProps {
    interactions: CRMInteraction[]; // A lista de dados já tratada pelo Hook
    loading?: boolean;              // Estado de carregamento para feedback visual
}

/**
 * Componente InteractionTimeline
 * * Responsável por renderizar a linha do tempo de interações do cliente.
 * Suporta visualização diferenciada para notas manuais vs. geradas por IA.
 */
export const InteractionTimeline: React.FC<InteractionTimelineProps> = ({ 
    interactions, 
    loading 
}) => {

    /**
     * HELPER: getIconInfo
     * Centraliza a lógica visual. Recebe o TIPO de interação e devolve:
     * 1. O Ícone correspondente (Componente React)
     * 2. A cor do texto (Tailwind class)
     * 3. A cor de fundo com opacidade (Tailwind class)
     */
    const getIconInfo = (type: InteractionType) => {
        switch (type) {
            case InteractionType.CALL:
                return { 
                    icon: FaPhone, 
                    color: 'text-blue-400', 
                    bg: 'bg-blue-400/10' 
                };
            case InteractionType.MEETING:
                return { 
                    icon: FaCalendarAlt, 
                    color: 'text-purple-400', 
                    bg: 'bg-purple-400/10' 
                };
            case InteractionType.EMAIL:
                return { 
                    icon: FaEnvelope, 
                    color: 'text-yellow-400', 
                    bg: 'bg-yellow-400/10' 
                };
            case InteractionType.WHATSAPP:
                return { 
                    icon: FaWhatsapp, 
                    color: 'text-green-400', 
                    bg: 'bg-green-400/10' 
                };
            case InteractionType.NOTE:
            default:
                // Fallback neutro para notas gerais
                return { 
                    icon: FaStickyNote, 
                    color: 'text-gray-400', 
                    bg: 'bg-gray-400/10' 
                };
        }
    };

    /**
     * HELPER: formatDate
     * Formata a data ISO do banco para o padrão brasileiro legível.
     * Ex: "10 de jan., 14:30"
     */
    const formatDate = (dateString: string) => {
        if (!dateString) return '';
        try {
            return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR', {
                day: '2-digit',
                month: 'short',
                hour: '2-digit',
                minute: '2-digit'
            }).format(new Date(dateString));
        } catch (e) {
            return dateString; // Fallback se a data vier inválida
        }
    };

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO: ESTADO DE LOADING ---
    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 animate-pulse">
                <FaStickyNote className="w-8 h-8 mb-2 opacity-50" />
                <span className="text-sm font-medium">Carregando histórico...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO: LISTA VAZIA ---
    if (interactions.length === 0) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 px-4 text-center text-gray-500 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-700 rounded-xl bg-gray-800/20">
                <FaStickyNote className="w-12 h-12 mb-3 opacity-20" />
                <p className="font-medium text-gray-300">Nenhuma interação registrada.</p>
                <span className="text-sm opacity-60 mt-1">
                    Use o campo abaixo para criar a primeira nota ou agendar uma tarefa.
                </span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO: LISTA DE DADOS ---
    return (
        <div className="space-y-6 relative">
            {interactions.map((interaction, index) => {
                // Extrai as cores e ícones baseados no tipo
                const { icon: Icon, color, bg } = getIconInfo(interaction.type);
                
                // Flag que indica se foi o Agente de IA que criou/tocou nesta nota
                const isAI = interaction.autoGenerated;
                
                // Verifica se é o último item para esconder a linha conectora
                const isLastItem = index === interactions.length - 1;

                return (
                    <div key={interaction.id} className="relative flex gap-4 group">
                        
                        {/* ========================================================
                            1. LINHA DO TEMPO (CONECTOR VERTICAL)
                           ======================================================== */}
                        {!isLastItem && (
                            <div 
                                className="absolute top-10 left-[19px] bottom-[-24px] w-0.5 bg-gray-800"
                                aria-hidden="true"
                            ></div>
                        )}

                        {/* ========================================================
                            2. BOLHA DO ÍCONE (Avatar da Interação)
                           ======================================================== */}
                        <div className={`
                            relative z-10 flex items-center justify-center 
                            w-10 h-10 rounded-full shrink-0 
                            ${bg} ${color} 
                            border border-gray-700/50 shadow-sm transition-transform hover:scale-105
                        `}>
                            <Icon size={16} />
                            
                            {/* Badge "Robozinho": Aparece apenas se for IA */}
                            {isAI && (
                                <div 
                                    className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 bg-accent-primary text-base-dark rounded-full p-1 border border-base-metallic shadow-md" 
                                    title="Gerado automaticamente pela IA"
                                >
                                    <FaRobot size={8} />
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        {/* ========================================================
                            3. CONTEÚDO (CARD DIREITO)
                           ======================================================== */}
                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            
                            {/* Cabeçalho do Card: Quem e Quando */}
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    {/* Nome do Autor: Se for IA, destaca com cor de accent */}
                                    <span className={`text-sm font-bold ${isAI ? 'text-accent-primary' : 'text-gray-200'}`}>
                                        {isAI ? 'Resumo Automático' : (interaction.userName || 'Usuário')}
                                    </span>
                                    
                                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">•</span>
                                    
                                    {/* Tipo da interação (Call, Email...) */}
                                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-500 bg-gray-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                                        {interaction.type}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                                
                                {/* Data Formatada */}
                                <time className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono">
                                    {formatDate(interaction.createdAt)}
                                </time>
                            </div>

                            {/* Corpo do Texto */}
                            <div className={`
                                p-4 rounded-lg border transition-colors duration-200
                                ${isAI ? 'bg-gray-800/80 border-accent-primary/20' : 'bg-gray-800/40 border-gray-700/50'}
                                hover:border-gray-600
                            `}>
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-300 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">
                                    {interaction.content}
                                </p>
                                
                                {/* ================================================
                                    4. ÁREA DE METADADOS DA IA (Tags)
                                    Renderiza tags apenas se a IA tiver analisado
                                   ================================================ */}
                                {interaction.aiMetadata?.analysis?.tags && interaction.aiMetadata.analysis.tags.length > 0 && (
                                    <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-700/50 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                        {interaction.aiMetadata.analysis.tags.map((tag: string) => (
                                            <span 
                                                key={tag} 
                                                className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400 bg-gray-700/50 rounded hover:bg-gray-700 hover:text-gray-200 cursor-default transition-colors"
                                            >
                                                #{tag}
                                            </span>
                                        ))}
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                );
            })}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListItem.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaBuilding, FaEnvelope, FaEdit, FaTrash, FaUndo } from 'react-icons/fa'; // Novos ícones
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';

interface ClientListItemProps {
    client: Client;
    onClick: (client: Client) => void;
    isSelected?: boolean;
    // --- NOVAS PROPS DE AÇÃO ---
    onEdit?: (client: Client) => void;
    onDelete?: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListItem: React.FC<ClientListItemProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    onClick, 
    isSelected = false,
    onEdit,
    onDelete
}) => {
    
    const baseClasses = "p-3 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition border group relative"; // Adicionado relative
    const stateClasses = isSelected 
        ? "bg-gray-800 border-accent-secondary shadow-md" 
        : "border-transparent hover:bg-gray-700 hover:border-gray-600";

    // Função auxiliar para evitar propagação do clique (selecionar o card)
    const handleAction = (e: React.MouseEvent, action?: (c: Client) => void) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (action) action(client);
    };

    return (
        <div 
            onClick={() => onClick(client)}
            className={`${baseClasses} ${stateClasses}`}
        >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                
                {/* Lado Esquerdo: Dados */}
                <div className="flex-1 pr-2">
                    <h3 className={`text-sm font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-white' : 'text-gray-200 group-hover:text-white'}`}>
                        {client.name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {client.document || 'Lead (Sem Doc)'}
                    </p>
                    
                    {/* Informação Secundária */}
                    <div className="mt-2 flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400">
                        {client.email ? (
                            <>
                                <FaEnvelope className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600 group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                                <span className="truncate max-w-[140px]">{client.email}</span>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <FaBuilding className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 text-gray-600" />
                                <span className="italic opacity-50">Sem contato</span>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                {/* Lado Direito: Status e Ações */}
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end space-y-2">
                    
                    {/* Badge de Status */}
                    <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                        client.isActive 
                            ? 'bg-green-900/20 text-green-400 border-green-900/30' 
                            : 'bg-red-900/20 text-red-400 border-red-900/30'
                    }`}>
                        {client.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                    </span>

                    {/* --- BOTÕES DE AÇÃO (Só aparecem no Hover ou se Selecionado) --- */}
                    <div className={`flex space-x-2 ${isSelected ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100'} transition-opacity duration-200`}>
                        
                        {/* Botão Editar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onEdit)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-accent-secondary hover:bg-gray-600 rounded transition"
                            title="Editar Cliente"
                        >
                            <FaEdit className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </button>

                        {/* Botão Excluir/Ativar */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={(e) => handleAction(e, onDelete)}
                            className={`p-1.5 rounded transition ${client.isActive 
                                ? 'text-gray-400 hover:text-danger hover:bg-gray-600' 
                                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-success hover:bg-gray-600'}`}
                            title={client.isActive ? "Desativar (Lixeira)" : "Reativar"}
                        >
                            {client.isActive ? <FaTrash className="w-3 h-3" /> : <FaUndo className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientListWidget.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaUser, FaPlus, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { Client } from '@/core/types/client';
// REMOVIDO: import { ClientModal } ... (O modal não deve mais existir aqui)
import { ClientSearchInput } from './ClientSearchInput';
import { ClientListItem } from './ClientListItem';
import { useClientMutation } from '@/core/hooks/useClientMutation';

interface ClientListWidgetProps {
    onSelectClient?: (client: Client | null) => void;
    refreshTrigger?: number;
    onNewClient: () => void;
    onEditClient: (client: Client) => void;
}

export const ClientListWidget: React.FC<ClientListWidgetProps> = ({ 
    onSelectClient,
    refreshTrigger = 0,
    onNewClient,
    onEditClient
}) => {
    
    const { 
        clients, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        searchTerm, 
        setSearchTerm, 
        refreshData 
    } = useClients(refreshTrigger);

    const { deleteClient, updateClient, isLoading: isMutating } = useClientMutation();
    const [selectedClientId, setSelectedClientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    
    // REMOVIDO: Estado local do modal (isModalOpen) - A página pai gerencia isso agora.

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    const handleSelect = (client: Client) => {
        if (selectedClientId === client.id) {
            setSelectedClientId(null);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(null);
            }
        } else {
            setSelectedClientId(client.id);
            if (onSelectClient) {
                onSelectClient(client);
            }
        }
    };

    // Handler direto: Apenas repassa a intenção para o Pai (CRMPage)
    const handleNewClient = () => {
        onNewClient(); 
    };

    const handleEdit = (client: Client) => {
        onEditClient(client);
    };

    const handleDelete = async (client: Client) => {
        const isActive = client.isActive;
        const actionText = isActive ? 'mover para a lixeira' : 'reativar';
        const confirmText = `Tem certeza que deseja ${actionText} o cliente "${client.name}"?`;

        if (confirm(confirmText)) {
            try {
                if (isActive) {
                    await deleteClient(client.id);
                } else {
                    await updateClient(client.id, { isActive: true });
                }
                refreshData();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Falha ao alterar status:", error);
                alert(`Erro ao ${actionText} cliente.`);
            }
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="col-span-3 bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-lg h-full">
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
                <h2 className="font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <FaUser className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> Clientes
                </h2>
                <button 
                    onClick={handleNewClient}
                    className="text-xs bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded flex items-center transition shadow-sm"
                    title="Adicionar Novo Cliente"
                >
                    <FaPlus className="mr-1" /> Novo
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Input de Busca */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                <ClientSearchInput 
                    value={searchTerm} 
                    onChange={setSearchTerm} 
                    isLoading={isLoading || isMutating} 
                />
            </div>

            {/* Lista */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-2 relative">
                
                {error && (
                    <div className="p-4 text-center text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-900/10 rounded-lg border border-red-900/30">
                        <FaExclamationTriangle className="mx-auto mb-2 w-5 h-5 opacity-50" />
                        <p>{error}</p>
                        <button onClick={() => refreshData()} className="mt-2 text-accent-primary hover:underline">
                            Tentar novamente
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}

                {!isLoading && !error && clients.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-500 text-sm">
                        <p>Nenhum cliente encontrado.</p>
                        {searchTerm && <p className="text-xs mt-1 opacity-70">Tente outro termo de busca.</p>}
                    </div>
                )}

                {clients.map(client => (
                    <ClientListItem
                        key={client.id}
                        client={client}
                        onClick={handleSelect}
                        isSelected={selectedClientId === client.id}
                        onEdit={handleEdit}
                        onDelete={handleDelete}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <div className="p-2 border-t border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 text-[10px] text-gray-500 text-center flex-shrink-0">
                {clients.length} registro(s) carregado(s)
            </div>

            {/* REMOVIDO: <ClientModal ... /> - Não renderizamos mais o modal aqui */}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/client-widget/ClientSearchInput.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaSearch } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface ClientSearchInputProps {
    value: string;
    onChange: (newValue: string) => void;
    isLoading?: boolean;
}

/**
 * @component ClientSearchInput
 * @description Campo de busca visual com ícone e estado de loading opcional.
 * * Pure Component: Não possui lógica de negócio, apenas UI.
 */
export const ClientSearchInput: React.FC<ClientSearchInputProps> = ({ 
    value, 
    onChange,
    isLoading = false
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-700 bg-base-metallic">
            <div className="relative">
                {/* Ícone: Alterna entre Lupa e Spinner se estiver carregando */}
                <div className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-500 w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {isLoading ? (
                        <span className="animate-spin h-3 w-3 border-2 border-accent-secondary border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
                    ) : (
                        <FaSearch />
                    )}
                </div>
                
                <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={value}
                    onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Buscar por nome, CPF ou CNPJ..." 
                    className="w-full bg-gray-900 text-sm text-white pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-secondary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-secondary transition-all placeholder-gray-500"
                />
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/communication/WhatsAppWidget.tsx

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaWhatsapp, FaQrcode, FaUnlink, FaCheckCircle, FaSync, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useMessaging } from '@/core/hooks/useMessaging';

/**
 * @component WhatsAppWidget
 * @description Gerencia a conexão com a instância do WhatsApp (Evolution API).
 * Exibe QR Code para pareamento ou Status de Conectado.
 */
export const WhatsAppWidget: React.FC = () => {
    
    const { 
        connectionStatus, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        checkStatus, 
        connectSession, 
        disconnectSession 
    } = useMessaging();

    // Verifica o status ao montar o componente
    useEffect(() => {
        checkStatus();
    }, [checkStatus]);

    // --- HANDLERS ---
    
    const handleConnect = () => {
        connectSession();
    };

    const handleDisconnect = () => {
        if (confirm("Tem certeza que deseja desconectar o WhatsApp deste Tenant?")) {
            disconnectSession();
        }
    };

    // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DE ESTADOS ---

    // 1. Loading
    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-gray-400 space-y-3 p-6">
                <FaSync className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-accent-primary" />
                <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Comunicando com o Servidor...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 2. Erro
    if (error) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-red-400 space-y-3 p-6 text-center">
                <FaExclamationTriangle className="w-8 h-8" />
                <span className="text-xs font-bold">{error}</span>
                <button 
                    onClick={() => checkStatus()} 
                    className="text-xs underline hover:text-white"
                >
                    Tentar Novamente
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 3. Conectado (Sucesso)
    if (connectionStatus?.isConnected) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-4 p-6 bg-green-900/10 rounded-lg border border-green-900/30">
                <div className="relative">
                    <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-green-900/50">
                        <FaWhatsapp className="w-8 h-8 text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-5 h-5 bg-green-400 border-2 border-base-metallic rounded-full"></div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="text-center">
                    <h3 className="text-white font-bold text-lg">WhatsApp Conectado</h3>
                    <p className="text-green-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide mt-1">
                        Instância: {connectionStatus.instanceName}
                    </p>
                </div>

                <button 
                    onClick={handleDisconnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 hover:bg-red-900/50 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition border border-gray-700 hover:border-red-800"
                >
                    <FaUnlink className="mr-2" /> Desconectar
                </button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // 4. Desconectado (Com ou Sem QR Code)
    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full space-y-6 p-6">
            
            {/* Se tiver QR Code, mostra ele. Senão, mostra o ícone genérico */}
            {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? (
                <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg shadow-xl animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Renderiza a string Base64 como imagem */}
                    <img 
                        src={connectionStatus.qrCode} 
                        alt="QR Code WhatsApp" 
                        className="w-48 h-48 object-contain" 
                    />
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gray-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-600 text-gray-500">
                    <FaQrcode className="w-8 h-8" />
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="text-center max-w-xs">
                <h3 className="text-white font-bold mb-1">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode ? 'Escaneie o QR Code' : 'WhatsApp Desconectado'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs">
                    {connectionStatus?.qrCode 
                        ? 'Abra o WhatsApp no seu celular e escaneie o código acima para conectar.' 
                        : 'Integre seu número para enviar mensagens diretamente pelo CRM.'}
                </p>
            </div>

            {!connectionStatus?.qrCode && (
                <button 
                    onClick={handleConnect}
                    className="flex items-center px-6 py-2.5 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg shadow-green-900/20 transition transform active:scale-95 text-sm"
                >
                    <FaWhatsapp className="mr-2 w-5 h-5" />
                    Gerar QR Code
                </button>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealEditor.tsx =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealHeaderForm.tsx =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/DealItemsTable.tsx =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deal-editor/index.ts =====




// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealForm.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealForm.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaSave, FaPlus, FaTrash, FaBox, FaWrench, FaExclamationTriangle, FaLink } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useClients } from '@/core/hooks/useClients';
import { useDeals } from '@/modules/crm/hooks/useDeals'; 
import { DealStatus, DealItemType, CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/types'; 
import { Product } from '@/modules/products/types'; 
import { ProductSelector } from '@/modules/products/components/ProductSelector'; 

interface DealFormProps {
    deal?: CRMDeal;
    initialStatus?: DealStatus;
    onSuccess: () => void;
    onCancel: () => void;
    
    // --- NOVO: Recebe o ID do funil vindo do Modal -> Kanban -> Container ---
    pipelineId?: string;
}

interface LocalItem {
    id: string;
    productId?: string;
    description: string;
    quantity: number;
    unitPrice: number;
    type: DealItemType;
}

export const DealForm: React.FC<DealFormProps> = ({ 
    deal, 
    initialStatus, 
    onSuccess, 
    onCancel,
    pipelineId // <--- Destrutarando a nova prop
}) => {
    
    const { clients } = useClients();
    const { createDeal, updateDeal, isLoading } = useDeals();

    const isEditing = !!deal;

    // Estados
    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [clientId, setClientId] = useState('');
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<DealStatus>(initialStatus || DealStatus.NEW);
    const [probability, setProbability] = useState(50);
    const [items, setItems] = useState<LocalItem[]>([]);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [newItem, setNewItem] = useState<Partial<LocalItem>>({
        description: '',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 0,
        type: DealItemType.SERVICE,
        productId: undefined
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (deal) {
            setTitle(deal.title);
            setClientId(deal.clientId);
            setStatus(deal.status);
            setProbability(deal.probability);
            
            if (deal.items && deal.items.length > 0) {
                setItems(deal.items.map(i => ({
                    id: i.id,
                    productId: i.productId || undefined,
                    description: i.description,
                    quantity: Number(i.quantity),
                    unitPrice: Number(i.unitPrice),
                    type: i.type
                })));
            } else {
                setItems([]);
            }
        } else {
            setStatus(initialStatus || DealStatus.NEW);
        }
    }, [deal, initialStatus]);

    const totalValue = items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (item.quantity * item.unitPrice), 0);

    const handleProductSelect = (product: Product) => {
        setNewItem({
            ...newItem,
            productId: product.id,
            description: product.title,
            unitPrice: product.promotionalPrice || product.price,
            type: product.type === 'SERVICE' ? DealItemType.SERVICE : DealItemType.PRODUCT
        });
    };

    const handleDescriptionChange = (text: string) => {
        setNewItem({
            ...newItem,
            productId: undefined,
            description: text
        });
    };

    const handleAddItem = () => {
        if (!newItem.description || newItem.unitPrice === undefined) return;
        setItems(prev => [...prev, {
            id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),
            productId: newItem.productId,
            description: newItem.description!,
            quantity: Number(newItem.quantity),
            unitPrice: Number(newItem.unitPrice),
            type: newItem.type || DealItemType.SERVICE
        }]);
        setNewItem({ description: '', quantity: 1, unitPrice: 0, type: DealItemType.SERVICE, productId: undefined });
    };

    const handleRemoveItem = (id: string) => {
        setItems(prev => prev.filter(i => i.id !== id));
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);

        if (!clientId) {
            setError("Selecione um cliente para prosseguir.");
            return;
        }

        const payload = {
            title,
            clientId,
            status,
            probability,
            items: items.map(i => ({
                productId: i.productId,
                description: i.description,
                quantity: i.quantity,
                unitPrice: i.unitPrice,
                type: i.type
            })),
            // --- CONEXÃO FINAL: Enviamos o pipelineId apenas na criação ---
            pipelineId: isEditing ? undefined : pipelineId 
        };

        try {
            if (isEditing && deal) {
                await updateDeal(deal.id, payload);
            } else {
                await createDeal(payload);
            }
            onSuccess();
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error(err);
            setError("Erro ao salvar oportunidade. Verifique os dados.");
        }
    };

    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col h-full">
            
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-1 pr-2 space-y-6">
                
                {error && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-red-900/30 border border-red-800 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-xs flex items-center">
                        <FaExclamationTriangle className="mr-2" /> {error}
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                     <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-accent-primary uppercase mb-1">Título da Oportunidade *</label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} required
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none"
                            placeholder="Ex: Projeto Site Institucional"
                        />
                    </div>
                     <div className="col-span-2 md:col-span-1">
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1">Cliente *</label>
                        <select 
                            value={clientId} onChange={e => setClientId(e.target.value)} required disabled={isEditing} 
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                            <option value="">Selecione...</option>
                            {clients.map(client => (
                                <option key={client.id} value={client.id}>{client.name}</option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-1">
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1">Fase do Funil</label>
                        <select value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value as DealStatus)} className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none">
                            {Object.values(DealStatus).map(s => (<option key={s} value={s}>{s.replace('_', ' ')}</option>))}
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-1">
                         <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1">Probabilidade (%)</label>
                         <input type="number" min="0" max="100" value={probability} onChange={e => setProbability(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-1 flex flex-col justify-end pb-2">
                        <div className="text-right">
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">Valor Total Estimado</span>
                            <div className="text-xl font-bold text-green-400">
                                {new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(totalValue)}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 2. GRID DE ITENS */}
                <div className="border-t border-gray-700 pt-4">
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-white mb-3 flex items-center">
                        <span className="bg-accent-secondary/20 text-accent-secondary p-1 rounded mr-2"><FaBox size={10}/></span> 
                        Itens do Negócio (Produtos/Serviços)
                    </h4>

                    <div className="bg-gray-800/50 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 overflow-visible">
                        <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
                            <thead className="bg-gray-900 text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold">
                                <tr>
                                    <th className="px-3 py-2">Tipo</th>
                                    <th className="px-3 py-2">Descrição / Produto</th>
                                    <th className="px-3 py-2 w-20">Qtd</th>
                                    <th className="px-3 py-2 w-28">Valor Unit.</th>
                                    <th className="px-3 py-2 w-10"></th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                                {items.map(item => (
                                    <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700/50 transition">
                                        <td className="px-3 py-2">
                                            {item.type === DealItemType.SERVICE ? <FaWrench className="text-blue-400" /> : <FaBox className="text-orange-400" />}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-white flex items-center">
                                            {item.productId && <FaLink className="mr-2 text-accent-primary" title="Vinculado ao Catálogo" />}
                                            {item.description}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-300">{item.quantity}</td>
                                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-gray-300">
                                            {new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(item.unitPrice)}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-center">
                                            <button type="button" onClick={() => handleRemoveItem(item.id)} className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-400">
                                                <FaTrash />
                                            </button>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))}

                                {/* Linha de Input */}
                                <tr className="bg-gray-800/80">
                                    <td className="px-2 py-2 align-top">
                                        <select 
                                            value={newItem.type} 
                                            onChange={e => setNewItem({...newItem, type: e.target.value as DealItemType})}
                                            className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-1 py-1.5 text-white text-[10px] w-full focus:outline-none"
                                        >
                                            <option value={DealItemType.SERVICE}>Serv.</option>
                                            <option value={DealItemType.PRODUCT}>Prod.</option>
                                        </select>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-2 py-2 align-top relative z-50">
                                        <ProductSelector 
                                            value={newItem.description || ''}
                                            onChange={handleDescriptionChange}
                                            onSelect={handleProductSelect}
                                            placeholder="Digite ou clique na lupa..."
                                        />
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-2 py-2 align-top">
                                        <input 
                                            type="number" min="1"
                                            value={newItem.quantity}
                                            onChange={e => setNewItem({...newItem, quantity: Number(e.target.value)})}
                                            className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white w-full focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                        />
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-2 py-2 align-top">
                                        <input 
                                            type="number" min="0" step="0.01"
                                            value={newItem.unitPrice}
                                            onChange={e => setNewItem({...newItem, unitPrice: Number(e.target.value)})}
                                            className="bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-white w-full focus:border-accent-primary focus:outline-none"
                                        />
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-2 py-2 text-center align-top">
                                        <button type="button" onClick={handleAddItem} className="bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-600 text-base-dark p-1.5 rounded shadow transition">
                                            <FaPlus size={10} />
                                        </button>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-gray-700 mt-4">
                <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-700 transition">Cancelar</button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={isLoading} className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-500 shadow-lg transition transform active:scale-95 flex items-center">
                    {isLoading ? 'Salvando...' : <><FaSave className="mr-2" /> {isEditing ? 'Salvar Alterações' : 'Criar Oportunidade'}</>}
                </button>
            </div>
        </form>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealModal.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/deals/DealModal.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaHandshake } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { DealForm } from './DealForm';
import { DealStatus, CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/types';

interface DealModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSuccess: () => void;
    defaultStatus?: DealStatus;
    
    // --- PROPS PARA EDIÇÃO ---
    dealToEdit?: CRMDeal; // Ajustei o nome para bater com o que vimos no CRMKanbanView (lá estava dealToEdit)
    
    // --- NOVO PROP: Contexto do Funil ---
    pipelineId?: string;
}

export const DealModal: React.FC<DealModalProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    onSuccess,
    defaultStatus,
    dealToEdit, // Usando o nome mais comum vindo do pai
    pipelineId  // <--- Recebendo o ID
}) => {
    
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const isEditing = !!dealToEdit;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-base-dark/70 backdrop-blur-sm z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center transition-all animate-fade-in p-4">
            
            <div 
                className="bg-base-metallic w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-600 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] transform scale-100 transition-all"
                onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                {/* Header */}
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-5 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 rounded-t-xl">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                        <div className="p-2 bg-accent-secondary/20 rounded-lg text-accent-secondary">
                            <FaHandshake size={20} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                                {isEditing ? 'Editar Oportunidade' : 'Nova Oportunidade'}
                            </h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Defina os detalhes e itens do negócio.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-700 p-2 rounded-full transition"
                    >
                        <FaTimes />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Corpo */}
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden p-6">
                    <DealForm 
                        deal={dealToEdit} // Passa o objeto de edição
                        initialStatus={defaultStatus}
                        
                        // --- AQUI ESTÁ A CONEXÃO ---
                        // Passamos o ID do funil para o formulário saber onde salvar
                        pipelineId={pipelineId}
                        
                        onSuccess={() => { onSuccess(); onClose(); }}
                        onCancel={onClose}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/kanban/DealCard.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/kanban/DealCard.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Draggable } from '@hello-pangea/dnd';
import { CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/types';
import { FaBuilding } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface DealCardProps {
    deal: CRMDeal;
    index: number;
    onEdit: (deal: CRMDeal) => void; // <--- NOVO
}

export const DealCard: React.FC<DealCardProps> = ({ deal, index, onEdit }) => {
    
    const formattedValue = new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', {
        style: 'currency',
        currency: 'BRL'
    }).format(Number(deal.value));

    return (
        <Draggable draggableId={deal.id} index={index}>
            {(provided, snapshot) => (
                <div
                    ref={provided.innerRef}
                    {...provided.draggableProps}
                    {...provided.dragHandleProps}
                    // --- AÇÃO DE CLIQUE DUPLO PARA EDITAR ---
                    onDoubleClick={() => onEdit(deal)} 
                    className={`
                        bg-gray-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 shadow-sm mb-3
                        hover:border-accent-secondary/50 transition-all group cursor-pointer
                        ${snapshot.isDragging ? 'shadow-2xl ring-2 ring-accent-primary rotate-2 z-50' : ''}
                    `}
                    style={{
                        ...provided.draggableProps.style,
                    }}
                    title="Clique duas vezes para editar"
                >
                    {/* Título e Probabilidade */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
                            {deal.title}
                        </h4>
                        {deal.probability > 0 && (
                            <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded 
                                ${deal.probability >= 70 ? 'bg-green-900/50 text-green-400' : 'bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-400'}
                            `}>
                                {deal.probability}%
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Cliente */}
                    <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400 mb-3">
                        <FaBuilding className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 opacity-50" />
                        <span className="truncate max-w-[150px]">
                            {deal.client?.name || 'Cliente Desconhecido'}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Footer */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-t border-gray-700/50 pt-2">
                        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-200 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-green-500 mr-1">$</span> {formattedValue}
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] text-white border border-gray-600">
                            <span className="font-bold">V</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </Draggable>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/kanban/KanbanBoard.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { DragDropContext, DropResult, Droppable } from '@hello-pangea/dnd';
import { CRMDeal, DealStatus } from '@/modules/crm/types';
import { IPipelineStage } from '@/modules/crm/types/pipeline.types';
import { KanbanColumn } from './KanbanColumn';

interface KanbanBoardProps {
    deals: CRMDeal[];
    stages: IPipelineStage[]; // <--- NOVO: Recebe as fases dinâmicas
    onDealMove: (dealId: string, newStageId: string, newStatus?: DealStatus) => void;
    onAddDeal: (status: DealStatus) => void;
    onEditDeal: (deal: CRMDeal) => void;
}

export const KanbanBoard: React.FC<KanbanBoardProps> = ({ 
    deals, 
    stages, 
    onDealMove, 
    onAddDeal, 
    onEditDeal 
}) => {
    const [isBrowser, setIsBrowser] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        setIsBrowser(true);
    }, []);

    const handleDragEnd = (result: DropResult) => {
        const { destination, source, draggableId } = result;

        if (!destination) return;
        if (destination.droppableId === source.droppableId && destination.index === source.index) return;

        // O droppableId agora é o ID do Stage (UUID), não mais o Status fixo
        const newStageId = destination.droppableId;
        
        // Encontramos o stage para saber qual o 'code' (status) correspondente
        const targetStage = stages.find(s => s.id === newStageId);
        const newStatus = targetStage?.code as DealStatus || DealStatus.NEW;

        onDealMove(draggableId, newStageId, newStatus);
    };

    if (!isBrowser) return <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-gray-500">Carregando Kanban...</div>;

    // Se não vier stages (ex: carregando ou erro), mostramos estado vazio
    if (!stages || stages.length === 0) {
        return <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-gray-500">Selecione um funil para visualizar.</div>;
    }

    return (
        <DragDropContext onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}>
            <div className="flex h-full space-x-4 overflow-x-auto pb-4 px-2">
                {/* Agora iteramos sobre os STAGES dinâmicos, não mais colunas fixas */}
                {stages.map(stage => {
                    // Filtra os deals desta coluna
                    // Nota: Idealmente filtramos pelo stageId. Se o backend ainda não salva stageId, usamos o status (code) como fallback
                    const columnDeals = deals.filter(d => 
                        d.currentStageId === stage.id || (!d.currentStageId && d.status === stage.code)
                    );
                    
                    const totalValue = columnDeals.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + Number(curr.value), 0);

                    return (
                        <KanbanColumn 
                            key={stage.id} // UUID do stage
                            id={stage.id}  // UUID usado pelo DragDrop
                            title={stage.name}
                            deals={columnDeals}
                            color={stage.color} // Cor vinda do banco
                            totalValue={totalValue}
                            onAddDeal={() => onAddDeal(stage.code as DealStatus)}
                            onEditDeal={onEditDeal}
                        />
                    );
                })}
            </div>
        </DragDropContext>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/kanban/KanbanColumn.tsx =====

import React from 'react';
import { Droppable } from '@hello-pangea/dnd';
import { CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/types'; // Removemos DealStatus, não precisamos dele aqui
import { DealCard } from './DealCard';
import { FaPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Ajustando para bater com o kanban.types.ts que definimos
interface KanbanColumnProps {
    id: string;          // UUID do Stage
    title: string;       // Nome do Stage
    color?: string;      // Cor da fase (Novo!)
    deals: CRMDeal[];
    totalValue: number;
    onAddDeal: () => void;
    onEditDeal: (deal: CRMDeal) => void;
}

export const KanbanColumn: React.FC<KanbanColumnProps> = ({ 
    id,
    title, 
    color = '#cccccc', // Default visual
    deals, 
    totalValue,
    onAddDeal,
    onEditDeal
}) => {
    
    const formattedTotal = new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', {
        style: 'currency',
        currency: 'BRL',
        notation: 'compact', 
    }).format(totalValue);

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full w-72 min-w-[288px] bg-base-metallic/30 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50 flex-shrink-0">
            
            {/* Header da Coluna com Indicador de Cor */}
            <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-700/50 bg-gray-800/40 rounded-t-xl flex justify-between items-center relative overflow-hidden">
                
                {/* Faixa colorida no topo baseada na configuração do Pipeline */}
                <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-[2px]" style={{ backgroundColor: color }}></div>

                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider truncate max-w-[120px]" title={title}>
                        {title}
                    </span>
                    <span className="bg-gray-700 text-gray-400 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">
                        {deals.length}
                    </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs font-bold text-accent-primary">
                    {formattedTotal}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Área de Drop - Agora usa o ID do Stage (UUID) */}
            <Droppable droppableId={id}>
                {(provided, snapshot) => (
                    <div
                        {...provided.droppableProps}
                        ref={provided.innerRef}
                        className={`flex-1 p-2 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar transition-colors flex flex-col ${
                            snapshot.isDraggingOver ? 'bg-gray-800/50' : ''
                        }`}
                    >
                        {deals.map((deal, index) => (
                            <DealCard 
                                key={deal.id} 
                                deal={deal} 
                                index={index} 
                                onEdit={onEditDeal}
                            />
                        ))}
                        {provided.placeholder}

                        {/* Botão de Adicionar Rápido */}
                        <button 
                            onClick={onAddDeal}
                            className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-center w-full py-2 border border-dashed border-gray-600 rounded-lg text-gray-500 hover:text-white hover:border-accent-secondary hover:bg-accent-secondary/10 transition group"
                        >
                            <FaPlus className="w-3 h-3 mr-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" /> 
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold">Novo Deal</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}
            </Droppable>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/components/pipeline-manager/PipelineDrawer.tsx =====

// src/modules/crm/components/pipeline-manager/PipelineDrawer.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaSave, FaStream } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { StageList, ConfigStage } from './StageList';

interface PipelineDrawerProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSave: (pipelineData: { name: string; stages: ConfigStage[] }) => void;
    initialData?: { name: string; stages: ConfigStage[] };
}
