// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/tabs/CategoriesTab.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
    FaPlus, 
    FaSearch, 
    FaEdit, 
    FaTrash, 
    FaTags 
} from 'react-icons/fa';

// Importando o Hook de Negócio existente (Reuso de Lógica)
import { useCategories } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategories';
import { useCategoryMutation } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategoryMutation';
import { Category } from '@/modules/categories/types';

// Importando o componente local do Drawer
import { CategoryFormDrawer } from './CategoryFormDrawer';

/**
 * @component CategoriesTab
 * @description Gestão de Categorias embarcada no módulo de Produtos.
 * @style Dark/Metallic (Design System)
 */
export const CategoriesTab: React.FC = () => {
    // Integração com a Camada de Dados (Leitura e Escrita)
    const { categories, isLoading } = useCategories();
    const { deleteCategory } = useCategoryMutation();
    
    // Estados Locais de Interface (UI State)
    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
    const [isDrawerOpen, setIsDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
    const [editingCategory, setEditingCategory] = useState<Category | null>(null);

    // Filtragem Local (Client-Side)
    const filteredCategories = categories?.filter(cat => 
        cat.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
    ) || [];

    // Handlers
    const handleNewCategory = () => {
        setEditingCategory(null); // Limpa seleção para criar novo
        setIsDrawerOpen(true);
    };

    const handleEditCategory = (category: Category) => {
        setEditingCategory(category); // Passa dados para edição
        setIsDrawerOpen(true);
    };

    const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
        // Futuro: Substituir por Modal de Confirmação customizado ou IA
        if (window.confirm('Tem certeza que deseja excluir esta categoria?')) {
            await deleteCategory(id);
        }
    };

    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-64 space-y-4 animate-pulse">
                <div className="h-8 w-8 bg-gray-700 rounded-full"></div>
                <span className="text-gray-400">Carregando categorias...</span>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* --- Toolbar (Busca e Ações) --- */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 justify-between items-center">
                
                {/* Barra de Busca Dark */}
                <div className="relative w-full md:w-96 group">
                    <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                        <FaSearch className="text-gray-500 group-focus-within:text-accent-primary transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Buscar categoria..."
                        className="block w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg leading-5 bg-gray-900 text-base-text placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:border-accent-primary sm:text-sm transition-colors"
                        value={searchTerm}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                    />
                </div>

                {/* Botão de Criação */}
                <button
                    onClick={handleNewCategory}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-accent-primary/90 text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all shadow-lg shadow-black/20 active:scale-95"
                >
                    <FaPlus className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Nova Categoria</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* --- Tabela de Categorias --- */}
            <div className="bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                
                {filteredCategories.length === 0 ? (
                    // Empty State Dark
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-16 text-center">
                        <div className="p-4 bg-gray-900 rounded-full mb-4 border border-gray-700">
                            <FaTags className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600" />
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-base-text">Nenhuma categoria encontrada</h3>
                        <p className="text-gray-400 mt-1">Crie categorias para organizar seus produtos.</p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                            
                            {/* THEAD */}
                            <thead className="bg-gray-900/50">
                                <tr>
                                    <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Nome</th>
                                    <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Status</th>
                                    <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-4 text-right text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Ações</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>

                            {/* TBODY */}
                            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700/50">
                                {filteredCategories.map((category) => (
                                    <tr key={category.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors group">
                                        
                                        {/* Coluna Nome */}
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                            <div className="flex items-center">
                                                <div className="h-8 w-8 flex-shrink-0 bg-gray-800 rounded flex items-center justify-center border border-gray-600 text-gray-400">
                                                    <span className="text-xs font-bold">{category.name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}</span>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="ml-4">
                                                    <div className="text-sm font-medium text-base-text group-hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors">
                                                        {category.name}
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>

                                        {/* Coluna Status */}
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border ${
                                                category.isActive 
                                                ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/20' 
                                                : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/20'
                                            }`}>
                                                <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${category.isActive ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500'}`}></span>
                                                {category.isActive ? 'Ativa' : 'Inativa'}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>

                                        {/* Coluna Ações */}
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-sm font-medium">
                                            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                                                <button 
                                                    onClick={() => handleEditCategory(category)}
                                                    className="text-gray-400 hover:text-accent-primary transition-colors" 
                                                    title="Editar"
                                                >
                                                    <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                                                </button>
                                                <button 
                                                    onClick={() => handleDelete(category.id)}
                                                    className="text-gray-400 hover:text-danger transition-colors" 
                                                    title="Excluir"
                                                >
                                                    <FaTrash className="w-4 h-4" />
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* Integração do Drawer de Formulário */}
            <CategoryFormDrawer 
                isOpen={isDrawerOpen} 
                onClose={() => setIsDrawerOpen(false)}
                initialData={editingCategory}
            />
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/tabs/CategoryFormDrawer.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaSave } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useCategoryMutation } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategoryMutation';
import { Category } from '@/modules/categories/types';

interface CategoryFormDrawerProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    initialData?: Category | null;
}

/**
 * @component CategoryFormDrawer
 * @description Painel lateral deslizante para Criação/Edição de Categorias.
 * @style Dark/Metallic (Design System)
 */
export const CategoryFormDrawer: React.FC<CategoryFormDrawerProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    initialData 
}) => {
    // Hooks de Mutação
    const { createCategory, updateCategory } = useCategoryMutation();
    const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

    // Estado do Formulário
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true);

    // Efeito: Popula o formulário ao abrir ou trocar o item selecionado
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            if (initialData) {
                // Modo Edição
                setName(initialData.name);
                // Garante que o toggle reflita o estado real do banco
                setIsActive(initialData.isActive);
            } else {
                // Modo Criação (Reset)
                setName('');
                setIsActive(true);
            }
        }
    }, [isOpen, initialData]);

    // Handler de Submissão
    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        if (!name.trim()) return;

        setIsSubmitting(true);
        try {
            // Montamos o payload completo para respeitar o Toggle da UI
            const payload = { name, isActive };

            if (initialData) {
                // Update: Obrigatório enviar isActive, senão não conseguimos inativar/ativar
                await updateCategory(initialData.id, payload);
            } else {
                // Create: Enviamos para respeitar caso o usuário crie já inativo
                await createCategory(payload);
            }
            onClose(); // Fecha o drawer após sucesso
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erro ao salvar categoria:', error);
            // Idealmente aqui entraria um Toast de erro
        } finally {
            setIsSubmitting(false);
        }
    };

    // Se não estiver aberto, não renderiza nada
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        // Overlay (Backdrop)
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex justify-end">
            {/* Fundo escurecido (Clica fora para fechar) */}
            <div 
                className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity" 
                onClick={onClose}
            ></div>

            {/* O Drawer em si (Painel Lateral) */}
            <div className="relative w-full max-w-md h-full bg-base-metallic border-l border-gray-700 shadow-2xl transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out flex flex-col">
                
                {/* Cabeçalho */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-5 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-900/50">
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-base-text">
                        {initialData ? 'Editar Categoria' : 'Nova Categoria'}
                    </h3>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700"
                    >
                        <FaTimes className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Corpo do Formulário (Scrollável) */}
                <form id="category-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 p-6 space-y-6 overflow-y-auto">
                    
                    {/* Campo: Nome */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <label htmlFor="name" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400">
                            Nome da Categoria <span className="text-accent-primary">*</span>
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            id="name"
                            value={name}
                            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Ex: Bebidas, Eletrônicos..."
                            className="block w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 rounded-lg text-base-text placeholder-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:border-transparent transition-all"
                            autoFocus
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Campo: Status (Toggle Visual) */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-gray-900/50 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
                        <div className="flex flex-col">
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-base-text">Status Ativo</span>
                            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Categorias inativas não aparecem no catálogo.</span>
                        </div>
                        
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setIsActive(!isActive)}
                            className={`relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-900 ${
                                isActive ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-gray-600'
                            }`}
                        >
                            <span
                                className={`inline-block h-4 w-4 transform rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${
                                    isActive ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-1'
                                }`}
                            />
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </form>

                {/* Rodapé (Ações Fixas) */}
                <div className="p-6 border-t border-gray-700 bg-gray-900/30 flex justify-end gap-3">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 bg-transparent border border-gray-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                        disabled={isSubmitting}
                    >
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        form="category-form"
                        disabled={isSubmitting || !name.trim()}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-accent-primary rounded-lg hover:bg-accent-primary/90 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-accent-primary disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all shadow-lg shadow-accent-primary/20"
                    >
                        {isSubmitting ? (
                            <>
                                <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-4 w-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent"></div>
                                <span>Salvando...</span>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <FaSave className="w-4 h-4" />
                                <span>{initialData ? 'Atualizar' : 'Salvar'}</span>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </button>
                </div>

            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/tabs/MovementsTab.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useStockMovements } from '@/modules/inventory/hooks/useStockMovements';
import { TransactionType } from '@/modules/inventory/types/InventoryTypes';
import { MovementsList } from '@/modules/inventory/components/MovementsList';

// Definimos APENAS o que este componente precisa receber do Pai
interface Props {
    variantId?: string; 
}

export function MovementsTab({ variantId }: Props) {
    // Usando o Hook Real que você me enviou
    const { fetchKardex, transactions, isLoading } = useStockMovements();

    // Ao montar a aba (ou mudar o ID), busca os dados reais no Backend
    useEffect(() => {
        if (variantId) {
            fetchKardex(variantId);
        }
    }, [variantId, fetchKardex]);

    // Função auxiliar renderizar Badge (Visual Dark/Neon)
    const renderTypeBadge = (type: TransactionType) => {
        const isEntry = type.startsWith('IN_');
        return (
            <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-bold border ${
                isEntry 
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/20' 
                : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/20'
            }`}>
                {isEntry ? 'Entrada' : 'Saída'}
            </span>
        );
    };

    if (isLoading) {
        return <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400 animate-pulse">Carregando histórico...</div>;
    }

    if (!transactions || transactions.length === 0) {
        // Empty State Dark
        return (
            <div className="p-8 text-center bg-base-metallic rounded-lg border border-dashed border-gray-700">
                <p className="text-gray-400">Nenhuma movimentação registrada para este produto.</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!variantId) {
        return (
            <div className="flex flex-col space-y-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-base-text">Kardex Global</h3>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                        Todas as Movimentações
                    </span>
                </div>
                
                {/* Reutiliza a lista do módulo de inventário (que já está Dark) */}
                <MovementsList />
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-base-text">Histórico de Movimentações (Kardex)</h3>
            
            {/* Tabela Dark/Metallic */}
            <div className="overflow-hidden bg-base-metallic border border-gray-700 rounded-lg shadow-lg">
                <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-700">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-900/50">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Data</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Tipo</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Local</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Qtd</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Doc</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-700">
                        {transactions.map((tx) => (
                            <tr key={tx.id} className="hover:bg-gray-700/30 transition-colors">
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-300 font-mono">
                                    {new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {renderTypeBadge(tx.type)}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-200">
                                    {tx.warehouse?.name || 'Local Indefinido'}
                                </td>
                                <td className={`px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-bold text-right font-mono ${
                                    Number(tx.quantity) > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'
                                }`}>
                                    {Number(tx.quantity) > 0 ? '+' : ''}{tx.quantity}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500">
                                    {tx.documentRef || '-'}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/components/tabs/WarehousesTab.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useWarehouses } from '@/modules/inventory/hooks/useWarehouses'; // Hook correto no módulo inventory
import { Warehouse } from '@/modules/inventory/types/InventoryTypes';
import { ManageWarehouseDrawer } from '@/modules/inventory/components/drawers/ManageWarehouseDrawer'; // Vamos implementar este modal a seguir

export function WarehousesTab() {
    // Integração com o Hook
    const { warehouses, isLoading, fetchWarehouses } = useWarehouses();
    
    // Estado local para controle do Modal
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectedWarehouse, setSelectedWarehouse] = useState<Warehouse | undefined>(undefined);

    // Carrega os dados ao montar a aba
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchWarehouses();
    }, [fetchWarehouses]);

    // Handlers
    const handleOpenCreate = () => {
        setSelectedWarehouse(undefined); // Limpa seleção para criar novo
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleOpenEdit = (warehouse: Warehouse) => {
        setSelectedWarehouse(warehouse); // Passa os dados para edição
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleCloseModal = () => {
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        // Opcional: Recarregar a lista se o modal retornar sucesso (geralmente o hook já atualiza o estado local)
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* --- CABEÇALHO DA ABA --- */}
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-base-text">Locais de Estoque</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                        Gerencie depósitos físicos (CDs, Lojas) e virtuais (Avarias, Reservas).
                    </p>
                </div>
                <button 
                    onClick={handleOpenCreate}
                    className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-accent-primary border border-transparent rounded-md font-medium text-white hover:bg-accent-primary/90 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-accent-primary transition-colors shadow-sm"
                >
                    <svg className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 -ml-1" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 6v6m0 0v6m0-6h6m-6 0H6" />
                    </svg>
                    Novo Local
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* --- CONTEÚDO DA LISTA --- */}
            {isLoading ? (
                // SKELETON LOADING (Dark Mode)
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="h-32 bg-gray-800 rounded-lg animate-pulse border border-gray-700"></div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            ) : warehouses.length === 0 ? (
                // EMPTY STATE (Dark Mode)
                <div className="text-center py-12 bg-base-metallic rounded-lg border border-dashed border-gray-700">
                    <div className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-500">
                        <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={1} d="M19 21V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v16m14 0h2m-2 0h-5m-9 0H3m2 0h5M9 7h1m-1 4h1m4-4h1m-1 4h1m-5 10v-5a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v5m-4 0h4" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="mt-2 text-sm font-medium text-base-text">Nenhum local cadastrado</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-400">Comece criando seu Depósito Principal ou Loja Física.</p>
                    <div className="mt-6">
                        <button 
                            onClick={handleOpenCreate}
                            className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 border border-accent-primary/20 shadow-sm text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/10 hover:bg-accent-primary/20 focus:outline-none"
                        >
                            Criar Primeiro Depósito
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            ) : (
                // LISTA DE DEPÓSITOS (GRID DARK)
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                    {warehouses.map((warehouse) => (
                        <div 
                            key={warehouse.id} 
                            className={`relative bg-base-metallic rounded-lg border shadow-sm transition-all hover:shadow-md ${
                                warehouse.isDefault ? 'border-accent-primary/50 ring-1 ring-accent-primary/20' : 'border-gray-700'
                            }`}
                        >
                            <div className="p-6">
                                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                                    <div className="flex items-center">
                                        <div className={`p-2 rounded-lg ${warehouse.isDefault ? 'bg-accent-primary/10 text-accent-primary' : 'bg-gray-800 text-gray-400'}`}>
                                            <svg className="w-6 h-6" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 21V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v16m14 0h2m-2 0h-5m-9 0H3m2 0h5M9 7h1m-1 4h1m4-4h1m-1 4h1m-5 10v-5a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v5m-4 0h4" />
                                            </svg>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="ml-4">
                                            <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-base-text flex items-center gap-2">
                                                {warehouse.name}
                                                {warehouse.isDefault && (
                                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-accent-primary/20 text-accent-primary border border-accent-primary/20">
                                                        Padrão
                                                    </span>
                                                )}
                                            </h3>
                                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                                                {warehouse.isActive ? 'Operacional' : 'Inativo'}
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div className="mt-4 border-t border-gray-700 pt-4 space-y-2">
                                    {/* Regra de Negócio Visual */}
                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                                        <span className="text-gray-400">Estoque Negativo:</span>
                                        {warehouse.allowsNegativeStock ? (
                                            <span className="text-yellow-400 font-medium flex items-center">
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-1" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                                                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z" />
                                                </svg>
                                                Permitido
                                            </span>
                                        ) : (
                                            <span className="text-emerald-400 font-medium flex items-center">
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-1" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                                                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                                                </svg>
                                                Bloqueado
                                            </span>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                    
                                    {/* Placeholder para Endereço */}
                                    {warehouse.addressInfo && (
                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                                            <span className="text-gray-400">Localização:</span>
                                            <span className="text-gray-300 truncate max-w-[150px]">
                                                {(warehouse.addressInfo as any).city || 'N/A'}
                                            </span>
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>

                                <div className="mt-6 flex gap-3">
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => handleOpenEdit(warehouse)}
                                        className="flex-1 inline-flex justify-center items-center px-4 py-2 border border-gray-600 shadow-sm text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-gray-300 bg-transparent hover:bg-gray-700 hover:text-white focus:outline-none"
                                    >
                                        Editar
                                    </button>
                                    <button 
                                        className="flex-1 inline-flex justify-center items-center px-4 py-2 border border-transparent shadow-sm text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/10 hover:bg-accent-primary/20 focus:outline-none"
                                    >
                                        Ver Estoque
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL (Renderizado Condicionalmente) --- */}
            {isModalOpen && (
                <ManageWarehouseDrawer 
                    isOpen={isModalOpen}
                    onClose={handleCloseModal}
                    initialData={selectedWarehouse}
                />
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/hooks/useProductMutation.ts =====

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';

// Import corrigido para modules conforme sua instrução
import { Product, ProductFormData } from '@/modules/products/types';

/**
 * Hook de Mutação para Produtos.
 * * Responsabilidade:
 * - Enviar dados de criação (POST) e atualização (PUT).
 * - Gerenciar estados de loading e erro da operação.
 * - Redirecionar após sucesso.
 */
export function useProductMutation() {
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const router = useRouter();

    /**
     * Cria um novo produto.
     * O payload (data) já vem tipado e formatado do ProductForm.
     */
    const createProduct = async (data: ProductFormData): Promise<Product | null> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            // O backend espera exatamente essa estrutura agora (com categoryId, fiscalGroupId, etc)
            const response = await api.post<Product>('/products', data);
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao criar produto:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao criar produto. Verifique os dados.';
            setError(msg);
            throw err;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Atualiza um produto existente.
     */
    const updateProduct = async (id: string, data: Partial<ProductFormData>): Promise<Product | null> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            const response = await api.put<Product>(`/products/${id}`, data);
            
            // Opcional: Forçar refresh da rota para atualizar dados em cache do Next.js
            router.refresh();
            
            return response.data;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao atualizar produto:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao salvar alterações.';
            setError(msg);
            throw err;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    /**
     * Remove um produto.
     */
    const deleteProduct = async (id: string): Promise<boolean> => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            await api.delete(`/products/${id}`);
            return true;
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('❌ Erro ao excluir produto:', err);
            const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Não foi possível excluir o produto. Verifique se há movimentações.';
            setError(msg);
            return false;
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return {
        createProduct,
        updateProduct,
        deleteProduct,
        isLoading,
        error
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/hooks/useProducts.ts =====

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
// Ajuste o import da API conforme sua estrutura real (se for @/services/api ou @/core/services/api)
import { api } from '@/core/services/api'; 
import { Product } from '../types';

/**
 * @hook useProducts
 * @description Gerencia a listagem, busca e estado de carregamento dos Produtos.
 * Local: src/modules/products/hooks/useProducts.ts
 */
export function useProducts(refreshTrigger: number = 0) {
    
    // Estados de Dados
    const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // Estados de Busca
    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
    const [debouncedTerm, setDebouncedTerm] = useState('');

    // 1. Debounce: Aguarda 500ms após parar de digitar
    useEffect(() => {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            setDebouncedTerm(searchTerm);
        }, 500);

        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }, [searchTerm]);

    // 2. Função de Fetching
    const fetchProducts = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            setIsLoading(true);
            setError(null);

            // Monta os parâmetros (query string ?q=...)
            const params = debouncedTerm ? { q: debouncedTerm } : {};

            // Ajuste a rota se necessário (ex: '/products' ou '/product')
            const response = await api.get('/product', { params });

            setProducts(response.data as Product[]);
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao buscar produtos:", err);
            const errorMessage = err.response?.data?.message || "Falha ao carregar produtos.";
            setError(errorMessage);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, [debouncedTerm]);

    // 3. Recarrega quando muda o termo (debounced) ou o trigger externo
    useEffect(() => {
        fetchProducts();
    }, [fetchProducts, refreshTrigger]);

    return {
        products,
        isLoading,
        error,
        searchTerm,      // Exposto para o Input value
        setSearchTerm,   // Exposto para o onChange
        refreshProducts: fetchProducts // Exposto com o nome que o ProductSelector espera
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/presentation/ProductCatalogue.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'; // Necessário para obter o contexto da URL
import { FaBoxOpen, FaPlus, FaSearch } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Hooks e Componentes
import { useProducts } from '@/modules/products/hooks/useProducts';
import { ProductListGrid } from '@/modules/products/components/ProductListGrid';
import { ProductForm } from '@/modules/products/components/ProductForm';
import { Product } from '@/modules/products/types';

/**
 * @component ProductCatalogue
 * @description View principal do catálogo.
 * Implementa o padrão Master-Detail: Lista em cima, Formulário embaixo.
 */
export const ProductCatalogue: React.FC = () => {
    // 1. Contexto: Captura o tenantId da rota atual (ex: /[tenantId]/dashboard/...)
    const params = useParams();
    const tenantId = params.tenantId as string;

    // 2. Estado local para forçar recarregamento se necessário
    const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);
    
    // 3. Hook de Dados (Consumo da API)
    const { 
        products, 
        isLoading, 
        error, 
        searchTerm, 
        setSearchTerm, 
        refreshProducts // CORREÇÃO: Nome atualizado conforme definição no hook useProducts.ts
    } = useProducts(refreshTrigger);

    // 4. Estados de UI (Seleção e Edição)
    const [selectedProduct, setSelectedProduct] = useState<Product | undefined>(undefined);
    const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);

    // Flag derivada: Devemos mostrar o formulário?
    const showForm = !!selectedProduct || isCreating;

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    // Usuário clicou no lápis no Grid
    const handleSelectProduct = (product: Product) => {
        setIsCreating(false);
        setSelectedProduct(product);
        // UX: Scroll suave até a área de edição
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.getElementById('product-form-container')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        }, 100);
    };

    // Usuário clicou em "Novo Item"
    const handleNewProduct = () => {
        setSelectedProduct(undefined);
        setIsCreating(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.getElementById('product-form-container')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        }, 100);
    };

    // Cancelar edição/criação
    const handleCancel = () => {
        setSelectedProduct(undefined);
        setIsCreating(false);
    };

    // Sucesso ao salvar (callback do form)
    const handleSuccess = () => {
        setRefreshTrigger(prev => prev + 1); // Força refresh da lista
        handleCancel(); // Fecha o form
    };

    const handleDelete = (id: string) => {
        // TODO: Conectar com hook de delete real
        console.log("Delete solicitado para ID:", id);
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 h-full min-h-0 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* --- CABEÇALHO --- */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-end flex-shrink-0">
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                        <FaBoxOpen className="mr-2 text-accent-secondary" /> Catálogo de Itens
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Visualização e cadastro manual de produtos.</p>
                </div>
                
                <button 
                    onClick={handleNewProduct}
                    className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 bg-accent-primary hover:bg-orange-600 text-base-dark font-bold rounded-lg shadow-md transition transform active:scale-95 text-sm"
                >
                    <FaPlus />
                    <span>Novo Item</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* --- LISTAGEM E BUSCA --- */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 min-h-[350px] flex-shrink-0">
                
                {/* Barra de Busca */}
                <div className="bg-base-metallic p-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 flex items-center">
                    <div className="pl-3 pr-2 text-gray-500">
                        {isLoading ? <span className="animate-spin text-accent-secondary">⏳</span> : <FaSearch />}
                    </div>
                    <input 
                        type="text"
                        value={searchTerm}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Buscar no catálogo (Nome, SKU...)"
                        className="bg-transparent border-none focus:ring-0 text-white w-full text-sm h-8 placeholder-gray-500"
                    />
                </div>

                {/* Grid de Produtos */}
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                    <ProductListGrid 
                        products={products}
                        isLoading={isLoading}
                        tenantId={tenantId}          // Passando o contexto obrigatório
                        onDelete={handleDelete}
                        onSelect={handleSelectProduct} // Habilita o modo de seleção local
                        selectedId={selectedProduct?.id} // Habilita o destaque visual
                    />
                    
                    {error && (
                        <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-400 bg-red-900/10 p-2 rounded text-center border border-red-900/30">
                            {error} - <button onClick={() => refreshProducts()} className="underline hover:text-white">Tentar novamente</button>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- FORMULÁRIO (ÁREA DE DETALHES) --- */}
            <div id="product-form-container" className="flex-1 min-h-[450px] flex flex-col transition-all duration-500 pb-1">
                {showForm ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex flex-col animate-fade-in-up">
                        {/* Indicador Visual */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 px-4 opacity-50">
                            <div className="w-0.5 h-4 bg-gray-600"></div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 tracking-widest">
                                {isCreating ? 'Novo Cadastro' : 'Editando Item Selecionado'}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* O Componente de Formulário */}
                        <ProductForm 
                            product={selectedProduct} // undefined = Criação, objeto = Edição
                            onSuccess={handleSuccess}
                            onCancel={handleCancel}
                        />
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    // Estado Vazio (Placeholder)
                    <div className="h-full border-t-2 border-dashed border-gray-800 bg-base-metallic/20 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-600">
                        <FaBoxOpen className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 opacity-20" />
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold opacity-50">Editor de Detalhes</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm max-w-md text-center opacity-40 mt-2">
                            Selecione um produto na lista acima para editar seus detalhes aqui mesmo.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/presentation/ProductContainer.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaChartPie, 
    FaBox, 
    FaExchangeAlt, 
    FaWarehouse, 
    FaTags, 
    FaFileInvoiceDollar 
} from 'react-icons/fa';

// --- Imports de Componentes Locais (Módulo Products) ---
import { OverviewTab } from '../components/tabs/OverviewTab';
import { CatalogTab } from '../components/tabs/CatalogTab';
import { MovementsTab } from '../components/tabs/MovementsTab';
import { WarehousesTab } from '../components/tabs/WarehousesTab';
import { CategoriesTab } from '../components/tabs/CategoriesTab'; // Novo Componente

// --- Imports Cross-Module (Reutilização de Componentes Existentes) ---
import { FiscalList } from '../../fiscal/components/FiscalList';
import { useAIContextInjector } from '@/core/ai/hooks/useAIContextInjector';

export type ProductViewType = 
    | 'OVERVIEW'
    | 'CATALOG'
    | 'MOVEMENTS'
    | 'WAREHOUSES'
    | 'CATEGORIES'
    | 'FISCAL';

interface ProductContainerProps {
    initialView?: ProductViewType;
}

/**
 * @component ProductContainer
 * @description Hub Principal de Supply Chain.
 * @style Dark/Metallic (Seguindo Design System)
 */
export const ProductContainer: React.FC<ProductContainerProps> = ({ 
    initialView = 'CATALOG' 
}) => {
    
    useAIContextInjector('SUPPLY_CHAIN');
    
    const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<ProductViewType>(initialView);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (initialView) {
            setActiveView(initialView);
        }
    }, [initialView]);

    const tabs = [
        { id: 'OVERVIEW', label: 'Visão Geral', icon: FaChartPie },
        { id: 'CATALOG', label: 'Catálogo', icon: FaBox },
        { id: 'MOVEMENTS', label: 'Movimentações', icon: FaExchangeAlt },
        { id: 'WAREHOUSES', label: 'Depósitos', icon: FaWarehouse },
        { id: 'CATEGORIES', label: 'Categorias', icon: FaTags },
        { id: 'FISCAL', label: 'Fiscal', icon: FaFileInvoiceDollar },
    ];

    const renderContent = () => {
        switch (activeView) {
            case 'OVERVIEW':
                return <OverviewTab />;
            case 'CATALOG':
                return <CatalogTab />;
            case 'MOVEMENTS':
                return <MovementsTab />;
            case 'WAREHOUSES':
                return <WarehousesTab />;
            case 'CATEGORIES':
                // Nova implementação: Componente nativo do módulo Products
                return <CategoriesTab />;
            case 'FISCAL':
                return (
                    // Wrapper estilizado com tema Metálico para listas externas (Fiscal ainda é legado)
                    <div className="bg-base-metallic rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-700 overflow-hidden min-h-[500px]">
                        <FiscalList />
                    </div>
                );
            default:
                return <OverviewTab />;
        }
    };

    return (
        // BASE: Fundo Escuro (#111827) e Texto Claro (#F3F4F6)
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full w-full bg-base-dark text-base-text">
            
            {/* HEADER: Fundo Escuro com Borda Sutil para separar do conteúdo */}
            <header className="bg-base-dark border-b border-gray-800 px-8 pt-8 pb-0 sticky top-0 z-20">
                <div className="flex flex-col space-y-6">
                    
                    {/* Título e Ações */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center pb-2">
                        <div>
                            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-base-text tracking-tight animate-fade-in-down">
                                {activeView === 'OVERVIEW' ? 'Supply Chain Tower' : 'Gestão de Materiais'}
                            </h1>
                            <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1 animate-fade-in">
                                {activeView === 'OVERVIEW' 
                                    ? 'Monitoramento estratégico de estoque e movimentações.' 
                                    : 'Controle operacional e cadastros de produtos.'}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Navegação de Abas */}
                    <nav className="flex space-x-8 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar">
                        {tabs.map((tab) => {
                            const isActive = activeView === tab.id;
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={tab.id}
                                    onClick={() => setActiveView(tab.id as ProductViewType)}
                                    className={`
                                        group flex items-center space-x-2 py-4 px-1 border-b-2 font-medium text-sm transition-all duration-200 whitespace-nowrap
                                        ${isActive 
                                            ? 'border-accent-primary text-accent-primary' // Ativo: Laranja Vibrante
                                            : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-base-text hover:border-gray-700' // Inativo: Cinza -> Claro
                                        }
                                    `}
                                >
                                    <tab.icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive ? 'text-accent-primary' : 'text-gray-500 group-hover:text-gray-300'}`} />
                                    <span>{tab.label}</span>
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </nav>
                </div>
            </header>

            {/* MAIN: Conteúdo com Fundo Transparente (herda base-dark) */}
            <main className="flex-1 p-8 overflow-y-auto animate-fade-in custom-scrollbar">
                {renderContent()}
            </main>

        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/types.ts =====

// src/modules/products/types.ts

// --- ENUMS (Mantidos do Original) ---

export enum ProductType {
    PHYSICAL = 'PHYSICAL',
    SERVICE = 'SERVICE',
    DIGITAL = 'DIGITAL',
    REAL_ESTATE = 'REAL_ESTATE',
    VEHICLE = 'VEHICLE'
}

export enum ProductCondition {
    NEW = 'new',
    REFURBISHED = 'refurbished',
    USED = 'used'
}

export enum ProductAvailability {
    IN_STOCK = 'in_stock',
    OUT_OF_STOCK = 'out_of_stock',
    PREORDER = 'preorder',
    BACKORDER = 'backorder'
}

// --- TIPOS DE METADATA (Mantidos do Original) ---

export interface PhysicalMetadata {
    weight_kg?: number;
    dimensions?: {
        height_cm: number;
        width_cm: number;
        depth_cm: number;
    };
    color?: string;
    material?: string;
    size?: string;
    gender?: 'male' | 'female' | 'unisex';
    age_group?: 'newborn' | 'infant' | 'toddler' | 'kids' | 'adult';
    model_code?: string;
}

export interface VehicleMetadata {
    year: number;
    mileage: number;
    fuel: 'GASOLINE' | 'ETHANOL' | 'DIESEL' | 'HYBRID' | 'ELECTRIC' | 'FLEX';
    transmission: 'MANUAL' | 'AUTOMATIC' | 'CVT' | 'DUAL_CLUTCH';
    plate_end?: string;
    color?: string;
    doors?: number;
    fipe_code?: string;
}

export interface RealEstateMetadata {
    property_type: 'HOUSE' | 'APARTMENT' | 'LAND' | 'COMMERCIAL' | 'FARM';
    area_total_m2: number;
    area_useful_m2?: number;
    bedrooms: number;
    bathrooms: number;
    suites?: number;
    parking_spaces: number;
    iptu_value?: number;
    condo_value?: number;
    features?: string[];
}

export interface ServiceMetadata {
    duration_minutes: number;
    is_remote: boolean;
    recurrence: 'NONE' | 'MONTHLY' | 'YEARLY';
    requirements?: string[];
}

export interface DigitalMetadata {
    file_size_mb?: number;
    file_format?: string;
    download_limit?: number;
    version?: string;
    requirements_os?: string;
}

// --- ENTIDADE PRINCIPAL (Interface do Objeto que vem da API) ---

export interface Product {
    id: string;
    tenantId: string;
    
    // Identificação
    title: string;
    shortDescription: string | null;
    longDescription: string | null;
    brand: string | null;
    
    // Mídia
    images: string[] | null; // URLs
    
    // --- VÍNCULOS E INTEGRAÇÕES (Alterado) ---
    // Substituído categoryPath por IDs relacionais
    categoryId: string;       // Vínculo obrigatório com Módulo de Categorias
    fiscalGroupId: string;    // Vínculo obrigatório com Módulo Fiscal
    
    // --- ESTOQUE (Novo) ---
    minStock: number;         // Ponto de pedido (alerta)
    currentStock: number;     // Saldo atual (apenas leitura ou via movimentação)

    // Códigos
    sku: string | null;
    gtin: string | null;
    mpn: string | null;

    // Valores e Estado
    price: number;
    promotionalPrice: number | null;
    condition: ProductCondition;
    availability: ProductAvailability;

    // Polimorfismo
    type: ProductType;
    
    // O campo flexível (Pode ser qualquer um dos tipos acima)
    metadata: 
        | PhysicalMetadata 
        | VehicleMetadata 
        | RealEstateMetadata 
        | ServiceMetadata 
        | DigitalMetadata 
        | null;

    isActive: boolean;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}

// --- INPUTS PARA FORMULÁRIOS (Payload para envio) ---

export interface ProductFormData {
    title: string;
    shortDescription: string;
    longDescription: string;
    brand: string;
    
    // --- VÍNCULOS E INTEGRAÇÕES (Alterado) ---
    // Novos campos de seleção
    categoryId: string;
    fiscalGroupId: string;
    
    // Estoque inicial na criação
    minStock: number;
    currentStock: number;
    
    // Campos de Marketplace
    gtin?: string;
    mpn?: string;
    
    sku: string;
    price: number;
    promotionalPrice?: number;
    condition: ProductCondition;
    availability: ProductAvailability;
    type: ProductType;
    images: string[]; 
    
    // O formulário vai preencher isso dinamicamente
    metadata: any; 
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/components/AppointmentModal.tsx =====

// src/modules/scheduler/components/AppointmentModal.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaCalendarPlus, FaClock, FaAlignLeft, FaTag, FaSave } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

interface AppointmentModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    /**
     * Datas pré-selecionadas ao clicar no calendário (Slot)
     */
    initialSlot?: { start: Date; end: Date };
    currentAppointment?: any; // Recebe o objeto do evento clicado (modo edição)
    /**
     * Callback para salvar (será conectado ao hook no próximo passo)
     */
    onConfirm: (data: { title: string; description: string; start: Date; end: Date }) => Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * @component AppointmentModal
 * @description Modal compacto e elegante para criação rápida de agendamentos.
 * Usa backdrop-blur para não perder o contexto do fundo.
 */
export const AppointmentModal: React.FC<AppointmentModalProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    initialSlot,
    currentAppointment,
    onConfirm 
}) => {
    
    // --- ESTADOS DO FORMULÁRIO ---
    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
    
    // Armazenamos como string para o input type="datetime-local"
    const [startStr, setStartStr] = useState('');
    const [endStr, setEndStr] = useState('');
    
    const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

    // Sincroniza o formulário quando o slot inicial muda
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            if (currentAppointment) {
                // MODO EDIÇÃO: Preenche com dados do agendamento existente
                setTitle(currentAppointment.title);
                setDescription(currentAppointment.description || '');
                
                // Converte as datas (que podem vir como string ISO do banco) para objeto Date
                const startDate = new Date(currentAppointment.startTime);
                const endDate = new Date(currentAppointment.endTime);

                setStartStr(format(startDate, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setEndStr(format(endDate, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
            } 
            else if (initialSlot) {
                // MODO CRIAÇÃO: Preenche com o slot clicado (vazio)
                setStartStr(format(initialSlot.start, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setEndStr(format(initialSlot.end, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setTitle('');
                setDescription('');
            }
        }
    }, [isOpen, initialSlot, currentAppointment]);

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        if (!title.trim() || !startStr || !endStr) return;

        setIsSaving(true);
        try {
            await onConfirm({
                title,
                description,
                start: new Date(startStr),
                end: new Date(endStr)
            });
            onClose();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro ao salvar:", error);
            // O tratamento de erro ideal seria um toast/alert aqui
        } finally {
            setIsSaving(false);
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        // OVERLAY: Fundo escuro translúcido com Blur (Suave)
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/20 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 transition-all duration-300">
            
            {/* CONTAINER: Compacto, Sombra Suave, Bordas Arredondadas */}
            <div 
                className="bg-base-metallic w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-700 transform scale-100 transition-all flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                
                {/* HEADER: Minimalista */}
                <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="p-2 bg-accent-secondary/20 text-accent-secondary rounded-lg">
                            <FaCalendarPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                            {currentAppointment ? 'Editar Agendamento' : 'Novo Agendamento'}
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 p-2 rounded-full transition"
                    >
                        <FaTimes />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* BODY: Formulário Clean */}
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-5">
                    
                    {/* Título */}
                    <div className="relative">
                        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-gray-500">
                            <FaTag className="text-xs" />
                        </div>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={title}
                            onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Título do compromisso (Ex: Reunião de Alinhamento)"
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-sm rounded-xl pl-9 pr-4 py-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary placeholder-gray-600 transition-all"
                            required
                            autoFocus
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Datas (Grid Lado a Lado) */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                                <FaClock className="mr-1.5" /> Início
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={startStr}
                                onChange={(e) => setStartStr(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-xs rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary"
                                required
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                                <FaClock className="mr-1.5" /> Fim
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={endStr}
                                onChange={(e) => setEndStr(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-xs rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary"
                                required
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Descrição */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                            <FaAlignLeft className="mr-1.5" /> Detalhes (Opcional)
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                            value={description}
                            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                            rows={3}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-sm rounded-xl px-4 py-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary resize-none placeholder-gray-600 scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-700"
                            placeholder="Adicione notas, pauta ou observações..."
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* FOOTER: Botões de Ação */}
                    <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end gap-3">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={onClose}
                            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-800 border border-transparent hover:border-gray-600 transition"
                        >
                            Cancelar
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={isSaving}
                            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-500 shadow-lg shadow-violet-900/20 transition transform active:scale-95 flex items-center disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                        >
                            {isSaving ? (
                                'Salvando...'
                            ) : (
                                <>
                                    <FaSave className="mr-2" /> 
                                    {currentAppointment ? 'Salvar Alterações' : 'Agendar'}
                                </>
                            )}
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/components/SchedulerWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/scheduler/components/SchedulerWidget.tsx

import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Calendar, View, Views, SlotInfo } from 'react-big-calendar';
import { FaSync, FaCalendarAlt } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { localizer } from '../utils/calendarLocalizer';
import { useScheduler } from '../hooks/useScheduler';
import { AppointmentType } from '../types';
import { AppointmentModal } from './AppointmentModal'; // <--- Importação do Modal

import 'react-big-calendar/lib/css/react-big-calendar.css';
import '../styles/calendar.css'; 

interface SchedulerWidgetProps {
    mode?: 'FULL' | 'COMPACT' | 'MINI';
    height?: number | string;
    clientId?: string; 
}

export const SchedulerWidget: React.FC<SchedulerWidgetProps> = ({ 
    mode = 'FULL',
    height = '100%',
    clientId
}) => {
    
    // Agora desestruturamos também o createAppointment do hook
    const { events, isLoading, isSyncing, syncGoogle, createAppointment, updateAppointment } = useScheduler({ clientId });
    const [appointmentToEdit, setAppointmentToEdit] = useState<any | undefined>(undefined);

    // --- ESTADOS DE CONTROLE ---
    const [view, setView] = useState<View>(mode === 'FULL' ? Views.MONTH : Views.AGENDA);
    const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date());

    // --- ESTADOS DO MODAL (NOVO) ---
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<{ start: Date; end: Date } | undefined>(undefined);

    const availableViews: View[] = mode === 'FULL' 
        ? [Views.MONTH, Views.WEEK, Views.DAY, Views.AGENDA]
        : [Views.AGENDA];
    
    const showToolbar = mode !== 'MINI';

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    const handleNavigate = useCallback((newDate: Date) => {
        setDate(newDate);
    }, []);

    const handleViewChange = useCallback((newView: View) => {
        setView(newView);
    }, []);

    // ADICIONE ESTA FUNÇÃO:
    const handleDoubleClickEvent = useCallback((calendarEvent: any) => {
        // O 'resource' contém o objeto Appointment original que veio da API
        const originalAppointment = calendarEvent.resource;
        
        setAppointmentToEdit(originalAppointment); // Guarda os dados para o form
        setIsModalOpen(true); // Abre o mesmo modal de criação
    }, []);

    // AJUSTE NO HANDLE DO MODAL (Para limpar o estado ao fechar):
    const handleCloseModal = () => {
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        setAppointmentToEdit(undefined); // Limpa para não abrir "sujo" na próxima
        setSelectedSlot(undefined);
    };

    /**
     * Disparado quando o usuário clica ou arrasta em um espaço vazio da agenda.
     */
    const handleSelectSlot = ({ start, end }: { start: Date; end: Date }) => {
        setAppointmentToEdit(null); // <--- GARANTIA: Garante que não é edição
        setSelectedSlot({ start, end });
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    /**
     * Callback de sucesso do Modal.
     * Chama o hook para persistir no backend.
     */
    const handleSaveAppointment = async (data: { title: string; description: string; start: Date; end: Date }) => {
    try {
        if (appointmentToEdit) {
            // MODO EDIÇÃO
            await updateAppointment({
                id: appointmentToEdit.id,
                title: data.title,
                description: data.description,
                start: data.start,
                end: data.end
            });
        } else {
            // MODO CRIAÇÃO
            await createAppointment({
                title: data.title,
                description: data.description,
                start: data.start,
                end: data.end
            });
        }
        // O modal fecha sozinho após o sucesso (via props do modal) ou você fecha manualmente aqui se preferir
        handleCloseModal();
    } catch (error: any) {
        const msg = error.response?.data?.message || 'Erro ao salvar agendamento.';
        alert(msg);
        throw error;
    }
};

    const eventStyleGetter = (event: any) => {
        const isBlock = event.resource?.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK;
        const style = {
            backgroundColor: isBlock ? '#374151' : '#8B5CF6', 
            borderRadius: '4px',
            opacity: 0.9,
            color: 'white',
            border: '0px',
            display: 'block'
        };
        return { style };
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 shadow-lg overflow-hidden relative">
            
            {mode === 'FULL' && (
                <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-800">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <FaCalendarAlt className="text-accent-primary" />
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                            {clientId ? 'Agenda do Cliente' : 'Agenda de Serviços'}
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    
                    <button 
                        onClick={syncGoogle}
                        disabled={isSyncing}
                        className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded transition border border-gray-600"
                    >
                        <FaSync className={`w-3 h-3 ${isSyncing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                        <span>{isSyncing ? 'Sincronizando...' : 'Sincronizar Google'}</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex-1 p-4 bg-gray-900" style={{ minHeight: mode === 'MINI' ? 200 : 500 }}>
                {isLoading ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500">
                        <FaSync className="animate-spin mr-2" /> Carregando agenda...
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <Calendar
                        localizer={localizer}
                        events={events}
                        startAccessor="start"
                        endAccessor="end"
                        style={{ height: '100%' }}
                        
                        view={view}
                        date={date}
                        onView={handleViewChange}
                        onNavigate={handleNavigate}
                        onDoubleClickEvent={handleDoubleClickEvent}
                        
                        // --- INTERATIVIDADE ---
                        selectable={true} // Habilita seleção
                        onSelectSlot={handleSelectSlot} // Handler do clique
                        scrollToTime={new Date(1970, 1, 1, 8)} // Rola para 08:00
                        longPressThreshold={10} // Melhor resposta no mobile
                        
                        views={availableViews}
                        toolbar={showToolbar}
                        eventPropGetter={eventStyleGetter}
                        messages={{
                            today: 'Hoje',
                            previous: 'Anterior',
                            next: 'Próximo',
                            month: 'Mês',
                            week: 'Semana',
                            day: 'Dia',
                            agenda: 'Lista',
                            date: 'Data',
                            time: 'Hora',
                            event: 'Evento',
                            noEventsInRange: 'Nenhum agendamento neste período.'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </div>

            {/* --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DO MODAL --- */}
            <AppointmentModal
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                initialSlot={selectedSlot}
                currentAppointment={appointmentToEdit}
                onConfirm={handleSaveAppointment}
            />
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/hooks/useScheduler.ts =====

// src/modules/scheduler/hooks/useScheduler.ts

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { Appointment, CalendarEvent, AppointmentType } from '../types';

export interface UseSchedulerProps {
    clientId?: string;
    resourceId?: string;
}

// Interface para Criação
interface CreateAppointmentInput {
    title: string;
    description?: string;
    start: Date;
    end: Date;
}

// Interface para Edição (ID é obrigatório)
interface UpdateAppointmentInput {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    description?: string;
    start: Date;
    end: Date;
}

export function useScheduler({ clientId, resourceId }: UseSchedulerProps = {}) {
    
    const [events, setEvents] = useState<CalendarEvent[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);

    // Converte o DTO da API para o formato do Calendário
    const mapToCalendarEvent = (apt: Appointment): CalendarEvent => ({
        id: apt.id,
        title: apt.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK ? 'Bloqueio Externo (Google)' : apt.title,
        start: new Date(apt.startTime),
        end: new Date(apt.endTime),
        resource: apt,
        isBlock: apt.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK
    });

    const fetchAppointments = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            setIsLoading(true);
            
            const params: Record<string, string> = {};
            if (clientId) params.clientId = clientId;
            if (resourceId) params.resourceId = resourceId;

            const response = await api.get('/scheduler/appointments', { params });
            const data = response.data as Appointment[];

            setEvents(data.map(mapToCalendarEvent));
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro ao buscar agenda:", error);
            setEvents([]);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, [clientId, resourceId]);

    /**
     * Cria um novo agendamento.
     */
    const createAppointment = async (input: CreateAppointmentInput) => {
        try {
            await api.post('/scheduler/appointments', {
                title: input.title,
                description: input.description,
                startTime: input.start.toISOString(),
                endTime: input.end.toISOString(),
                clientId: clientId || undefined, 
                resourceId: resourceId || undefined
            });

            await fetchAppointments();
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao criar agendamento:", error);
            throw error;
        }
    };

    /**
     * @method updateAppointment
     * @description Atualiza um agendamento existente via PATCH.
     */
    const updateAppointment = async (input: UpdateAppointmentInput) => {
        try {
            // Rota PATCH que criamos no Backend
            await api.patch(`/scheduler/appointments/${input.id}`, {
                title: input.title,
                description: input.description,
                startTime: input.start.toISOString(),
                endTime: input.end.toISOString(),
            });

            // Recarrega para refletir a mudança
            await fetchAppointments();

        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao atualizar agendamento:", error);
            throw error; // Lança para o componente tratar (ex: exibir erro de conflito)
        }
    };

    const syncGoogle = async () => {
        try {
            setIsSyncing(true);
            await api.post('/scheduler/sync');
            await fetchAppointments(); 
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro no sync:", error);
        } finally {
            setIsSyncing(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchAppointments();
    }, [fetchAppointments]);

    return {
        events,
        isLoading,
        isSyncing,
        syncGoogle,
        createAppointment,
        updateAppointment, // <--- Nova função exposta
        refresh: fetchAppointments
    };
}
